The resulting corpus holds 1034 validated vulnerabilities across 80 projects and 63 weakness categories, with edit locality similar to real CVE patches under a real-versus-real noise floor. Fine-tuning on trajectories collected over this corpus improves SEC-bench patch repair by +3.3 to +14.7 points, in all six configurations of three model scales and two teachers, with the 31B student reaching its GPT-5.4-mini teacher, 72.7% against 74.0%. These gains generalize out of distribution to PatchEval, a corpus containing other programming languages, where every configuration also improves and the 31B student passes its teacher.

</details>


### [132] [StepJack: Benchmarking Computer-Use Agent Safety Against Multi-Step Indirect Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06477)
*Zhuoxin Zhan,Akbar Rafiey,Avery Ma,Leila Pishdad,Layla El Asri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对计算机使用代理（CUA）的新型攻击类别——多步间接提示注入，通过将恶意目标自动分解为多个看似无害的子步骤并分布在代理导航路径上的多个页面中，构建了包含480个测试样本的StepJack安全基准，实验表明该攻击在部分CUA上可将攻击成功率提升多达31.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用代理（CUAs）正面临来自间接提示注入（一种将对抗性指令植入网页等环境中的攻击）日益严重的威胁。现有研究主要关注单步间接提示注入，而多步攻击的威胁尚未被系统研究，缺乏相应的安全基准来评估和改进CUA的防御能力。

Method: 作者提出了一条自动化流水线，能够在"分解子步骤的执行必须实现原始对抗目标"以及"每个子步骤的无害性最优化"双重约束下，将对抗目标自动分解为多个子步骤。基于该流水线构建了StepJack基准（含480个测试用例），并在此基准上系统评估了六个最先进的CUA模型。

Result: 在固定分解深度下，多步攻击在六个CUA中的三个上显著提升了攻击成功率（ASR），最高提升达31.2个百分点（如GPT-5.4-mini从单步的41.7%提升到三步的72.9%）；在五个能可靠遵循参考链的CUA上平均ASR从单步的31.3%提升到三步的36.9%。

Conclusion: 多步间接提示注入是一种比单步攻击更具威胁性的新型攻击方式，其分解策略能有效绕过CUA现有的安全机制。StepJack作为首个针对该攻击类别的安全基准，为未来CUA安全研究与防御策略开发提供了重要的评估工具。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) face a growing threat from indirect prompt injection, where adversarial instructions are planted in the environment such as web pages. In this paper, we introduce multi-step indirect prompt injection, a new attack class against CUAs in which the adversarial goal is decomposed into multiple innocuous-looking sub-steps and distributed across a chain of pages referenced along the agent's navigation path. We develop a pipeline to automatically decompose an adversarial goal under the constraint that the execution of the decomposed sub-steps must achieve the original goal while optimizing the innocuousness of each decomposed sub-step. With this pipeline, we build StepJack, a CUA safety benchmark with 480 test examples. On this benchmark, we evaluate six state-of-the-art CUAs and find that at a fixed decomposition depth, multi-step attacks raise attack success rate (ASR) on three of six CUAs, by up to 31.2 points (e.g., GPT-5.4-mini: 41.7% at single-step to 72.9% at three-step); averaged over the five CUAs that can reliably follow the reference chain (all but EvoCUA-32B), ASR rises from 31.3% at single-step to 36.9% at three-step. Dataset and code are available at https://github.com/BorealisAI/StepJack.

</details>


### [133] [Canonicalization Failures as a Recurring Vulnerability Class: Representation Divergence in Cryptographic Systems and Its Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06508)
*Arslan Brömme*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统化梳理了跨生态系统中因"字节表示与语义对象之间的非唯一映射"而引发的一类密码学攻击面（如交易可延展性、非确定性值编码、消息可延展性、哈希链可延展性等），指出它们共同的根因是"唯一性条件被违反"，并从表示机制、计算理论基础和可执行的审查流程三个层面进行整合，提出了一种预防性的规范化审查义务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码系统在字节层操作但表达语义对象，两者之间的翻译很少是唯一的。当唯一性未被强制时，只要哈希、签名、重放保护或共识身份依赖于表示形式，攻击面就会打开。这类失败在多个生态系统中被独立发现并各自命名（如交易可延展性、消息可延展性等），但缺乏一个跨生态系统的统一框架来追踪其共同根源。

Method: 本文并非发现新现象，而是将分散的研究发现系统化整理，贡献有三：一是按表示机制（而非受影响系统）进行分类；二是在经典canonicalization安全与表示理论/可计算性理论基础之间建立桥梁；三是转化为一套可应用的审查流程，包括规范化审查义务（一系列审查步骤）、字段类型分类和操作边界模型，用于预防性地识别风险。

Result: 作者用具体案例验证了这一失败类别，将其与不属于表示问题的安全事件进行了区分。研究展示了"强制唯一性可以降低一类失败的可利用性"，但明确限制其声称范围：它既不替代其他保护措施，也不对狭义上的密码学安全性做出任何声明。

Conclusion: 这些安全隐患的共同根因是唯一性条件被违反，沿两个方向展开：对象侧多重编码（一个对象多种有效编码）与代码侧语义坍缩（一个编码对应多个对象）。通过系统化的分类和可操作的审查程序，这类风险可以在预防阶段被识别。但该工作仅是对失败类的可利用性降低，不能替代更广泛的防护措施。

Abstract: Cryptographic systems operate on bytes but mean semantic objects. The translation between the two is rarely unique. Where this uniqueness is not enforced, an attack surface opens up as soon as a hash, a signature, replay protection, or consensus identity depends on the representation. The same class of failure has been discovered independently and named locally across many ecosystems, as transaction malleability, non-deterministic value encoding, message malleability, or hash chain malleability, without its common root being tracked as a cross-ecosystem grid. This work organizes the scattered findings systematically: it shows that they are instances of one violated uniqueness condition, along two basic directions: multiple valid codes for one object (object-side multiple representation) or one code for multiple objects (code-side semantic collapse). The contribution is explicitly not the discovery of the phenomenon, but is threefold: the systematization by representation mechanism rather than by affected system, the bridge between classical canonicalization security and a representation- and computability-theoretic foundation, and the translation into an applicable review procedure (a canonicalization obligation with a sequence of review steps, a field-type classification, and an operational boundary model) with which the risk can be recognized preventively. We substantiate the class with worked-out cases, delimit it against incidents that are not representation problems, and deliberately keep the claims to what is demonstrable: enforced uniqueness can reduce the exploitability of a failure class, but it neither replaces further protective measures or makes any statement about cryptographic security in the narrower sense

</details>


### [134] [Model Confidence Under Answer-Preserving Attacks: An Informativeness-Manipulability Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06571)
*Reza Khanmohammadi,Ivan Brugere,Simerjot Kaur,Charese H. Smiley,Kundan Thind,Mohammad M. Ghassemi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究系统性地证明了在保证生成答案逐字节不变的白盒图像攻击下，视觉-语言系统的置信度读出是不可靠的监督信号。通过可达性假设、均匀幅度证书等理论框架，以及跨4个模型、3个基准、5个置信度通道和2个防御估计器的实证攻击，作者展示了在全部84个估计器-单元格组合中均能构造可行扰动，并将对抗判别力降至或低于答案字符串准确率先验（0.617）的水平。隐藏状态干预和激活空间复制实验表明，置信度扰动可在表征层面而非仅通过对抗图像实现。四种防御族均未提供稳健替代方案。在置信度门控模拟中，转移攻击导致高达84.8%的先前被拒错误答案被接受，重加权后接受准确率在转移场景下8/12单元格低于无门控基线，直接攻击下12/12全部低于基线。结论：在研究威胁模型和预算下，置信度是完整性敏感而非固有稳健的监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署中的视觉-语言系统常常基于置信度对答案进行门控决策，因此置信度的稳健性直接关系到系统监督的安全性。作者希望考察在极端条件下——即攻击只能修改图像而不能改变最终生成的答案文本——置信度读出是否依然可信。这是对下游监督机制安全性的核心质疑。

Method: 采用白盒、仅图像的对抗攻击，约束条件为生成的答案逐字节不变。理论层面提出可达性假设与均匀幅度证书（uniform amplitude certificate），建立"不可移动读出器"与答案字符串准确率先验（0.617）之间的性能下界关系。实证层面跨4个视觉-语言模型、3个VQA基准、5个部署置信度通道和2个防御估计器，执行直接或替代目标（surrogate-aimed）攻击；同时进行坐标级正确性标签感知攻击、隐藏状态干预以及开放文本模型的激活空间复制实验，最终进行置信度门控模拟。

Result: 直接或替代目标攻击在全部84个估计器-单元格组合中均成功构造了满足可行性条件的扰动，反驳了均匀幅度证书。协调正确性标签感知攻击在全部60个部署通道单元格中（包括所有59个开始时高于答案字符串下限的单元格）将对抗判别力降至或低于0.617的下限。隐藏状态干预和激活空间复制显示表征层面可诱导相当的置信度移动。四种防御族在各自具体评估下均未建立稳健替代方案。在置信度门控模拟中，协调token概率攻击转移到隐藏状态门控时，最多84.8%的先前被拒错误答案被接受；重加权至自然正确率先验后，转移攻击下8/12单元格、直接门控定向攻击下12/12单元格的接受准确率均低于无门控基线。

Conclusion: 在所研究的威胁模型和攻击预算下，视觉-语言系统的置信度读出本质上属于完整性敏感（integrity-sensitive）信号，而非固有稳健的监督机制。这意味着基于置信度的门控决策在面临能够保持答案不变的图像扰动时，无法提供可靠的下游安全保障。

Abstract: Deployed vision-language systems often gate their answers on confidence, making confidence robustness relevant to oversight. We study confidence readouts under white-box, image-only attacks constrained to preserve the generated answer byte-identically. Under a reachability assumption, an unmovable readout cannot outperform the answer-string accuracy prior, whose pooled value is 0.617. Independently of that assumption, a uniform amplitude certificate below a measurable threshold guarantees adversarial discrimination above the same floor. Across four vision-language models, three visual question answering benchmarks, five deployed confidence channels and two defense estimators, direct or surrogate-aimed attacks produce itemwise feasible perturbations that refute this uniform certificate in all 84 estimator-by-cell combinations. Coordinated correctness-label-aware attacks drive adversarial discrimination to or below the answer-string floor in all sixty deployed-channel cells, including all fifty-nine that begin above it. Hidden-state interventions and an open-ended text-model activation-space replication show that comparable confidence movement can be induced at the representation level rather than only through adversarial images. None of four tested defense families establishes a robust alternative under the specific evaluation applied to it. In a confidence-gated simulation, a coordinated token-probability attack transferred to a hidden-state gate causes up to 84.8% of previously rejected wrong answers to become accepted. After reweighting to each benchmark's natural correctness prevalence, accepted accuracy falls below the no-gate baseline in eight of twelve cells under transfer and all twelve under a direct gate-aimed attack. Under the studied threat model and budget, confidence is therefore an integrity-sensitive rather than intrinsically robust oversight signal.

</details>


### [135] [WhiteNet: Robust Identification of Overlapping IEEE 802.11 Signals Across Unseen Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06581)
*Ildi Alla,Vincent Lenders*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WhiteNet 是一个针对 I/Q 样本中深度学习分类器对信道变化敏感问题的框架。它通过光谱白化（频谱白化）预处理抑制频率选择性衰落，并利用合成的重叠混合器进行预训练，减少对昂贵的多发射器现场数据采集的依赖。在公开的过空 IEEE 802.11 数据上，WhiteNet 显著缩小了因未见信道条件造成的精度差距，参数仅为先前最先进模型的 1/7.7，并可蒸馏为紧凑变体用于功率受限的边缘设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于深度学习的分类器在 I/Q 样本上训练时，能对重叠的 IEEE 802.11 信号实现高精度协议识别，但当部署时信道条件与训练时不同，其性能会急剧下降。需要一种方法来处理信道可变性问题，同时避免依赖昂贵的大规模多发射器现场数据采集。

Method: WhiteNet 框架采用两个核心手段：(1) 光谱白化，这是一个基于物理原理的预处理步骤，用于抑制频率选择性衰落，同时保留协议判别特征；(2) 合成重叠混合器，包含物理上准确的每发射器信道模型和共享接收器信号链，用于预训练而无需大量现场数据收集。

Result: 在公开的过空 IEEE 802.11 数据上，WhiteNet 大幅缩小了由未见信道条件引起的精度差距，同时参数量仅为先前最先进方法的 1/7.7（即节省了约 87% 的参数）；该方法还可选地进行知识蒸馏，生成紧凑变体以适应功率受限的边缘设备上的粗粒度频谱感知。

Conclusion: WhiteNet 通过物理驱动的光谱白化和合成数据预训练，有效解决了深度学习信号分类在信道变化条件下的性能退化问题，在精度与模型效率之间取得了良好平衡，适合部署于资源受限的边缘环境。

Abstract: Deep learning (DL) classifiers trained on I/Q samples achieve high accuracy for IEEE 802.11 protocol identification of overlapping signals, but their performance degrades sharply when channel conditions at deployment differ from those encountered during training. We present WhiteNet, a framework that addresses the problem of channel variability in I/Q samples. The central idea is spectral whitening, a physics-grounded preprocessing step that suppresses frequency-selective fading while preserving protocol-discriminative features. To reduce dependence on costly multi-transmitter over-the-air captures for training, we complement it with a synthetic overlap mixer featuring a physically accurate per-transmitter channel and shared-receiver signal chain for pre-training without extensive field data collection. On public over-the-air IEEE 802.11 data, WhiteNet closes a substantial portion of the accuracy gap caused by unseen channel conditions while using 7.7 times fewer parameters than the prior state of the art, and optionally distills to compact variants for coarse spectrum awareness on power-constrained edge devices.

</details>


### [136] [From Documentation to Zero-day Vulnerabilities: LLM-Driven Fuzzing of JavaScript Engines in PDF Readers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06641)
*Suyue Guo,Stijn Pletinckx,Tianle Yu,Yigitcan Kaya,Saad Ullah,Wenbo Guo,Christopher Kruegel,Giovanni Vigna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PDFuzzer是一个新颖的PDF引擎模糊测试工具，利用LLM构建上下文无关语法并推断API调用关系，通过约束求解器生成复杂的API调用序列，在三大主流PDF阅读器上显著超越现有模糊测试工具，发现31个零日漏洞并获得厂商赏金。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PDF阅读器模糊测试工具仅依赖涉及单个API调用的简单测试用例，导致覆盖率有限，可能遗漏需要多个API调用序列才能触发的漏洞。

Method: PDFuzzer首先使用大型语言模型(LLM)从JavaScript API手册和执行轨迹中提取规范，构建上下文无关语法并推断API调用之间的关系；然后基于语法和关系，使用约束求解器生成具体的API调用序列用于模糊测试。

Result: 在Adobe Acrobat Reader、Foxit PDF Reader和PDF-XChange Editor三个主流PDF阅读器上，PDFuzzer比现有工具(TypeOracle、Favocado、Cooper)和LLM模糊器(Fuzz4All、naive LLM)覆盖率最高提升48%，发现31个零日漏洞（从信息泄露到任意代码执行），各流水线阶段LLM准确率达93-98%。

Conclusion: PDFuzzer通过LLM辅助的语法构建和API关系推断，结合约束求解生成复杂API序列，显著提升了PDF引擎模糊测试的覆盖率和漏洞发现能力；消融实验验证了各组件的必要性，所有漏洞已通过协调披露流程提交厂商并获得赏金。

Abstract: Existing fuzzers for PDF readers rely on simple test cases that involve only individual API calls, leading to limited coverage and potentially missing vulnerabilities that require sequences of API calls. To address these limitations, we propose PDFuzzer, a novel PDF engine fuzzer that automatically generates complex and meaningful API call sequences. PDFuzzer first uses a Large Language Model (LLM) to construct context-free grammars and infer the relationships between individual API calls from specifications extracted from JavaScript API manuals and execution traces. Based on the grammars and relationships, PDFuzzer employs a constraint solver to generate concrete API call sequences for fuzzing. Our experiments show that PDFuzzer significantly outperforms state-of-the-art PDF fuzzers (TypeOracle, Favocado, and Cooper) and LLM-based fuzzers (Fuzz4All, naive LLM) on three mainstream PDF readers: Adobe Acrobat Reader, Foxit PDF Reader, and PDF-XChange Editor. PDFuzzer achieves up to 48% higher coverage than existing tools and identifies 31 zero-day vulnerabilities in these readers, from information leakage to arbitrary code execution. Our ablation study validates the necessity of each component, including LLMs, which achieve high accuracy across all pipeline stages (93-98%). We disclosed all vulnerabilities to the vendors via a coordinated vulnerability disclosure process and received bug bounties.

</details>


### [137] [CyberLLM: A Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Autonomous Detection and Guarded Response in Automotive Cybersecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06651)
*Nenad Petrovic,Oussama Jeddou,Feres Ben Fraj,Vahid Zolfaghari,Fengjunjie Pan,Andre Schamschurko,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CyberLLM，一个基于多智能体LLM编排的框架，在形式化运行时安全护栏下自主检测并修复软件定义车辆（SDV）的漏洞。检测结合确定性层（正则、AST分析、拓扑图检查）与LLM精化，决策智能体聚合发现并选择分级响应，每个动作都经过四个上下文安全属性和独立对齐预言机验证。在9个真实汽车ECU模块（C/C++/Rust）的47个分层漏洞基准上，确定性层以完美精度覆盖34%漏洞，加入LLM精化后覆盖率约达70%（F1 0.83），且干净对照组零误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件定义车辆（SDV）大幅扩展了汽车的攻击面（源代码、运行时日志、部署拓扑），同时安全约束禁止自主代理在无监督下行动。需要一种既能自主检测和修复漏洞，又能满足汽车行业严格安全合规要求的框架。

Method: CyberLLM采用多智能体LLM编排架构：1）确定性检测层（正则规则、AST分析器、拓扑图检查）提供高召回基础；2）LLM精化与完整性检查补充高精度推理；3）决策智能体聚合发现、按人类资产分类法标注，并选择分级响应；4）签名跨会话记忆和重新规划反馈不断提升置信度；5）每个动作须通过四个上下文安全属性及独立动作对齐预言机的验证，被拒动作触发升级和重新规划；6）对称攻击管线生成并重放漏洞利用，使攻防两侧在同一场景下得到测试。

Result: 在9个独立汽车ECU模块（C/C++/Rust）的47个分层漏洞及干净对照组基准上：始终开启的确定性层以完美精度覆盖34%标记漏洞；加入LLM精化与完整性检查后覆盖率提升至约70%，F1为0.83，且在干净对照组上产生零误报。

Conclusion: 研究表明，当LLM代理被包裹在确定性、可审计的安全包络中时，能够执行有用的自主网络防御。该框架在保持高精度的同时将漏洞覆盖率提升到约70%，证明了确定性安全护栏与LLM智能结合的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Software-Defined Vehicles (SDVs) expand the automotive attack surface across source code, runtime logs, and deployment topologies, while safety constraints forbid autonomous agents from acting without oversight. This paper presents CyberLLM, a multi-agent, LLM-orchestrated framework that autonomously detects vulnerabilities and executes remediations under a formal, runtime safety guard. Detection combines a deterministic layer (regex rules, AST analyzers, and topology graph checks) with an LLM refinement pass, so a high-recall floor is complemented by high-precision reasoning. A decision agent aggregates findings, tags them with a human-centric asset taxonomy, and selects a tiered response, ratcheting its confidence with signed cross-session memory and re-planning feedback. Every action is validated against four contextual security properties and an independent action-alignment oracle before it is allowed to run, and refused actions trigger escalation and re-planning. A symmetric attack pipeline generates and replays exploits so both sides can be exercised on the same scenarios. On an independent, ground-truthed benchmark of nine original automotive ECU modules in C, C++, and Rust seeding 47 layered vulnerabilities plus clean controls, the always-on deterministic layer covers 34\% of the labeled vulnerabilities at perfect precision, and adding the grounded LLM refinement and completeness passes roughly doubles coverage to about 70\% (F1 $0.83$) while producing zero false positives on the clean controls. The results indicate that LLM agents can perform useful autonomous cyber-defense when wrapped in a deterministic, auditable safety envelope.

</details>


### [138] [Policy-Masked Private Experts: Auditable and Reversible Capability Access Control in Sparse MoE Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06690)
*Zhuoheng Huang,Mukesh Singh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Policy-Masked Private Experts (PMPE)机制：冻结预训练稀疏MoE模型，训练独立专家分支，并通过可信授权在top-k路由前选择公开或私有专家池，从而实现对"哪些新训练参数可被前向传播访问"的细粒度执行控制。实验在Qwen3-30B-A3B和DeepSeek-V2-Lite上验证：未经授权请求的私有专家执行为零，同时私有分支可在工具使用等任务上带来显著效用提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型访问控制大多在行为层面（如提示词过滤、输出审查）进行限制，但所有请求仍可访问相同的计算资源。作者研究一个不同的系统问题：受信任的授权机制能否决定哪些新训练的参数在前向传播中可被访问？即实现"执行控制"与"任务效用"的分离。

Method: 方法分为三个部分：(1) 架构层面：冻结预训练的稀疏MoE模型，训练一个不相交的私有专家分支，在top-k路由之前根据授权策略选择公开或私有专家池；(2) 安全声明：在声明的可信计算基（TCB）下，未经授权的请求不执行任何私有专家；(3) 评估设计：在Qwen3-30B-A3B（三个BF16种子，更新全部32个私有专家）和DeepSeek-V2-Lite上测试执行控制与任务效用的分离，包括64个对抗场景、96个deny/fail-closed事件、11,616条路由行的hook验证，以及前瞻性冻结基准上的工具使用任务评估。

Result: 技术上，未经授权的私有专家执行为零；独立hook精确匹配11,616条私有路由行；allow-deny-allow恢复精确。效用上，Qwen私有分支在工具使用上提升5.0pp（p=0.03125）和21.3pp（95% CI [13.3, 29.3], p=0.000031）；三盲模型评估者确认18.7pp的外部效用（95% CI [9.3, 28.0]）。对比中，参数量匹配的LoRA有相似外部效用但post-hoc请求门会残留1,225次适配器调用，而PMPE为零。DeepSeek复现路由不变性并提升27.0pp。密封评估结果接近中性。

Conclusion: 研究支持对已训练参数路径进行可审计、可逆的控制，同时表明有用的迁移仍然是分布依赖的。该方法实现了执行控制与任务效用的有效分离，但需要注意其安全声明有明确的TCB边界，且不声称公开模型缺乏相同语义能力。

Abstract: Most language-model access controls regulate behavior while leaving the same computation available to every request. We study a different systems question: can trusted authorization determine which newly trained parameters are reachable by the forward pass? Policy-Masked Private Experts freezes a pretrained sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model, trains a disjoint expert branch, and selects the public or private pool before top-k routing. The resulting claim is narrow but testable: under the declared trusted computing base (TCB), an unauthorized request executes no private expert. It does not imply that the public model lacks the same semantic capability.
  We test this separation between execution control and task utility in Qwen3-30B-A3B and DeepSeek-V2-Lite. Three Qwen BF16 seeds update all 32 private experts while the public fingerprint remains unchanged. Across 64 adversarial scenarios and 96 deny/fail-closed events, unauthorized private execution is zero; independent hooks exactly match 11,616 routed private rows and allow-deny-allow recovery is exact. On two prospectively frozen Qwen benchmarks, the private branch improves exact tool use by 5.0 percentage points (pp) (five versus zero discordances; one-sided Holm p = 0.03125, corresponding two-sided exact p = 0.0625) and 21.3 pp (percentile-bootstrap 95% CI [13.3, 29.3], Holm p = 0.000031). Three arm-blinded model evaluators retain a positive external effect of 18.7 pp (95% CI [9.3, 28.0]). A parameter-matched Lora has similar external utility, but a post-hoc request gate leaves 1,225 adapter calls under deny; the disjoint expert branch leaves none. DeepSeek reproduces the route invariant and gains 27.0 pp. A valid sealed evaluation is near-neutral. These results support auditable, reversible control over a trained parameter path, while showing that useful transfer remains distribution dependent.

</details>


### [139] [Tight Security for BBS Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06724)
*Rutchathon Chairattana-Apirom,Dennis Hofheinz,Stefano Tessaro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了BBS签名方案的具体安全性。在正面，证明了在每条消息最多被签名一次（含去随机化签名的常见实际用例）的情况下，存在针对q-SDH假设的紧致安全归约；在负面，通过元归约论证证明若允许多次签同一条消息，则不存在紧致的代数归约到q-SDH及其变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BBS签名是实际隐私保护认证系统的底层代数签名方案，正在被W3C和IRTF标准化。虽然BBS+已有紧致安全证明，但BBS的效率更高且近期安全证明（Tessaro和Zhu, EUROCRYPT '23）推动了标准化和工业采用向BBS倾斜，然而该证明并不紧致。此外，近期密码分析（ASIACRYPT '25）表明紧致归约到q-SDH是我们所能期望的最佳结果。因此，理解BBS与BBS+之间紧致性差距是否本质至关重要。

Method: 本文采用双管齐下的方法：（1）正面构造：针对每条消息最多签名一次的场景，特别是覆盖去随机化签名的实际用例，给出一个新的紧致归约到q-SDH假设的证明；（2）负面结果：利用元归约（meta-reduction）论证，证明若允许对同一消息生成多个签名，则任何代数归约到q-SDH（及其变体）都不可能实现紧致性。

Result: 在正面，本文成功构建了BBS在每条消息至多签名一次条件下的紧致安全归约，闭合了与BBS+之间的紧致性差距（至少在该实际场景下）。在负面，通过元归约方法证明了在允许多次签同一消息的一般情况下，任何代数归约到q-SDH都不可能紧致，从而将紧致性差距定位为固有而非技术性。

Conclusion: 本文完整地回答了BBS与BBS+之间紧致性差距的本质问题：在去随机化或多消息签名场景下，BBS可以实现紧致安全性；但在允许多重签名的通用场景下，紧致归约到q-SDH本质上不可能。这为标准化和工业界的方案选择提供了重要的理论依据。

Abstract: This paper studies the concrete security of BBS signatures (Boneh, Boyen, Shacham, CRYPTO '04; Camenisch and Lysyanskaya, CRYPTO '04), a popular algebraic construction of digital signatures which underlies practical privacy-preserving authentication systems and is undergoing standardization by the W3C and IRTF.
  Schäge (Journal of Cryptology '15) gave a tight standard-model security proof under the q-SDH assumption for a less efficient variant of the scheme, called BBS+--here, q is the number of issued signatures. In contrast, the security proof for BBS (Tessaro and Zhu, EUROCRYPT '23), also under the q-SDH assumption, is \emph{not} tight. Nonetheless, this recent proof shifted both standardization and industry adoption towards the more efficient BBS, instead of BBS+, and for this reason, it is important to understand whether this tightness gap is inherent. Recent cryptanalysis by Chairattana-Apirom and Tessaro (ASIACRYPT '25) also shows that a tight reduction to q-SDH is the best we can hope for.
  This paper closes this gap in two different ways. On the positive end, we show a novel tight reduction for BBS in the case where each message is signed at most once--this case covers in particular the common practical use case which derandomizes signing. On the negative end, we use a meta-reduction argument to prove that if we allow generating multiple signatures for the same message, then {\em no} algebraic reduction to q-SDH (and its variants) can be tight.

</details>


### [140] [Retrieval-Constrained Policy Optimization for Attack Technique Extraction from Cyber Threat Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06778)
*Jiayun Zhang,Junshen Xu,Zejun Xie,Yi Fan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TTP-R1，一个两阶段框架，结合检索增强的监督微调（SFT）与基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），用于将网络威胁情报（CTI）文本自动映射到MITRE ATT&CK技术。混合检索器缩小标签空间，微调LLM选择正确技术，然后通过Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）和分解奖励直接监督预测技术集合的精确率、召回率和输出格式。在四个CTI基准上取得最佳平均F1，子技术级F1较Claude Sonnet 4.5（带检索增强）提升7.4个百分点，且以8B参数单GPU部署时速度快28倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将CTI文本映射到MITRE ATT&CK技术对结构化威胁分析至关重要，但手动标注成本高且不可扩展。现有方法各有不足：多标签分类器难以应对严重的类别不平衡和大标签空间；基于LLM的方法（检索管道和微调生成器）优化token级目标，将技术标注视为序列生成而非集合预测，缺乏对预测集合正确性和完整性的直接监督。

Method: TTP-R1采用两阶段框架。第一阶段：混合检索器将大量标签空间缩小为候选集，微调LLM学习选择正确技术；第二阶段：应用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO），使用分解奖励直接监督预测技术集合的精确率、召回率和输出格式。

Result: 在四个CTI基准上取得最佳平均F1；子技术级F1比Claude Sonnet 4.5（带检索增强）高7.4个百分点；作为8B参数模型在单GPU上部署时运行速度快28倍。

Conclusion: TTP-R1通过将集合预测目标引入强化学习奖励设计，有效解决了CTI技术标注中标签空间大、多标签冲突的问题，在性能上超越了检索增强的大型商用模型，同时在推理效率上具有显著优势。

Abstract: Mapping cyber threat intelligence (CTI) text to MITRE ATT&CK techniques is essential for structured threat analysis, yet manual annotation is costly and does not scale. The ATT&CK taxonomy comprises several hundred attack techniques, and a single CTI passage may describe multiple techniques, making accurate and complete extraction challenging. Existing automated approaches fall short in different ways: multi-label classifiers struggle with severe class imbalance and the large label space, while LLM-based methods--retrieval pipelines and fine-tuned generators--optimize token-level objectives that treat technique annotation as sequence generation rather than set prediction, lacking direct supervision on whether the predicted technique set is correct and complete. We propose TTP-R1, a two-stage framework that combines retrieval-augmented supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with reinforcement learning using verifiable rewards (RLVR). A hybrid retriever first narrows the large label space to a candidate set, and a fine-tuned LLM learns to select the correct techniques. We then apply Group Relative Policy Optimization with a decomposed reward that directly supervises the precision, recall, and output format of the predicted technique set. Across four CTI benchmarks, TTP-R1 achieves the best average F1, improving sub-technique-level F1 by 7.4 percentage points over Claude Sonnet 4.5 with retrieval augmentation, while running 28x faster when served as an 8B-parameter model on a single GPU.

</details>


### [141] [LoRAScan: Detecting Backdoor Prompts in Low-Rank Adapters for Large Language Models via Down-Projection Activation Spikes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06795)
*Doniyorkhon Obidov,Honggang Yu,Xiaolong Guo,Kaichen Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LoRAScan是首个适配器感知的LoRA后门防御方法，在推理时检测并拒绝包含触发器的恶意输入，无需修改适配器参数。通过在部署前识别约5%的低方差LoRA插入位点并在推理时监控这些位点的下投影激活值尖峰，LoRAScan能以约98.49%的恶意输入拒绝率和极低的干净输入误报率检测后门攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA适配器的供应链安全问题日益突出：不可信的适配器可能携带后门，在包含隐藏触发器的输入下生成有害内容。现有防御存在不足：适配器无关的方法通过融合适配器稀释了后门信号；适配器感知的方法要么训练防御性适配器去修复被后门化的基座模型（解决的是相反问题），要么依赖分类器标记整个适配器为可疑并要求单独缓解。这些方法都忽视了后门适配器中触发器输入所产生的独特潜在空间签名。

Method: LoRAScan观察到仅有约5%的LoRA插入位点在干净输入上保持稳定，但当触发器出现时这些位点的LoRA下投影激活值会出现高度集中的尖峰。该方法在模型部署前识别这些低方差插入位点，并在推理期间持续监控它们。当检测到异常激活尖峰时，LoRAScan直接拒绝该输入，无需修改适配器参数。

Result: 在标准LLM后门基准测试中，LoRAScan能够拒绝约98.49%的恶意输入，同时在干净输入上保持较小的错误率，并在多种评估设置下优于现有的防御方法。

Conclusion: LoRAScan通过利用后门触发输入在LoRA插入位点产生的独特激活签名，首次实现了推理时对触发器输入的实时检测和拒绝，为适配器感知的LoRA供应链安全提供了新的防御范式。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) enables efficient specialization and distribution of large language models through compact adapters. However, untrusted adapters introduce a supply-chain threat: a backdoored adapter can cause a model to generate harmful content, malicious code, political propaganda, or covert advertisements when an input contains a hidden trigger. Adapter-agnostic defenses merge the adapter with the base model, which dilutes backdoor signals and reduces detection performance. Existing adapter-aware methods do not address how to safely use a potentially backdoored adapter. Instead, they either train a defensive adapter to repair a backdoored base model, addressing the inverse problem rather than securing the adapter itself, or rely on a classifier that flags the entire adapter as suspicious and requires separate mitigation. These methods overlook the distinct latent-space signatures produced by trigger-bearing inputs in backdoored adapters.
  We introduce LoRAScan, the first adapter-aware defense that detects and rejects trigger-bearing inputs at inference time without modifying adapter parameters. Our key observation is that a small subset of LoRA insertion sites, approximately 5%, remains stable across clean inputs but exhibits highly concentrated spikes in LoRA down-projection activations when a trigger is present. LoRAScan identifies these low-variance insertion sites before model deployment and monitors them during inference. Across standard LLM backdoor benchmarks, LoRAScan rejects approximately 98.49 of malicious inputs with a small error rate on clean inputs, outperforming existing defenses across diverse evaluation settings.

</details>


### [142] [On a General Theoretical Framework for Radio Frequency Fingerprint-Based Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06805)
*Yuanyu Zhang,Jianing Wang,Shuangrui Zhao,Pinchang Zhang,Yulong Shen,Xiaohong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的RF指纹（RFF）理论框架，将RFF建模与认证属性分析相结合，从底层解释RFF为何及如何作为可靠的无线设备身份，涵盖RFF的诱导、演化和观测过程，以及其唯一性、稳定性、可区分性和不可伪造性等认证属性的检验方法，并据此探讨通信与认证的协同设计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然基于RF指纹的无线设备认证已在多种数据集和场景中得到广泛研究，但缺乏一个基础理论来解释RFF为何及如何能够作为可靠的设备身份，这严重阻碍了该认证技术的实际应用。

Method: 提出一个通用理论框架，包含两个核心过程：(1) RFF建模过程——沿着发射机-信道-接收机链路揭示RFF如何被诱导、演化及观测；(2) 认证属性分析过程——从唯一性、稳定性、可区分性和不可伪造性四个维度检验RFF的可信性。通过将RFF的形成/演化与这些认证属性建立关联，形成闭环的理论支撑。

Result: 框架成功建立了RFF形成/演化过程与四项认证属性之间的内在联系，为理解RFF认证的可靠性提供了坚实的理论基础；同时基于框架的理论洞见，讨论了通信与认证协同设计的问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的理论框架填补了RFF认证领域基础理论缺失的空白，为RFF认证技术的实践应用提供了理论指引，并为通信-认证协同设计指明了方向。

Abstract: While radio frequency fingerprint (RFF)-based wireless device authentication has been widely studied across different datasets and scenarios, there still lacks a fundamental theory to explain why and how RFF can serve as a reliable device identity, significantly hindering the practical application of such an authentication technology. In this article, we integrate the RFF modeling with authentication property analysis to propose a general theoretical framework to facilitate the development of such a theory. The RFF modeling process reveals how RFFs are induced, evolved and observed along the transmitter-channel-receiver chain, built upon which, the authentication property analysis process then outlines how the trustworthiness of an RFF should be examined in terms of its uniqueness, stability, distinguishability, and unforgeability. By linking the RFF formation/evolution to these authentication properties, the framework offers a solid foundation for understanding why and how RFF-based authentication is trustworthy in practice. We also discuss the communication-authentication co-design issue based on the theoretical insights from the proposed framework.

</details>


### [143] [POKEx: Performance analysis of POKE-key exchange and SIDH-variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06826)
*Hyeonhak Kim,Suhri Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对基于POKE的密钥交换与SIDH变体（M-SIDH、terSIDH、CSIDH）进行了性能对比分析。通过将Eurocrypt 2025提出的POKE方案适配为密钥交换算法，在NIST安全级别1下，POKE-based KEM比terSIDH快约21.21倍，比CSIDH快约64.97倍，表明POKE是当前最有前景的基于同源的密钥交换方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SIDH因2022年Castryck和Decru提出的密钥恢复攻击而在经典计算机上被证明存在多项式时间攻击，其安全性被削弱。虽然提出了MSIDH、MD-SIDH、bin/terSIDH等对策，但这些方案仍面临性能瓶颈。因此，需要寻找既有安全性保障又具有高效性能的同源密码方案。

Method: 将POKE方案（Basso和Maino在Eurocrypt 2025提出的同源公钥加密方案，结合SIDH类协议与高维同源）适配为密钥交换算法，并针对NIST安全级别1，与M-SIDH、terSIDH和CSIDH进行基准性能对比测试。

Result: 在NIST安全级别1下，POKE-based KEM的运行速度约为terSIDH的21.21倍，约为CSIDH的64.97倍，性能显著优于其他同源密钥交换候选方案。

Conclusion: POKE-based KEM是目前最有前景的同源密码密钥交换方案，在保持安全性的同时实现了远超现有同源方案的性能表现。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a comparative performance analysis of the POKE-based key exchange and SIDH variants. SIDH gained attention for its small key size and efficient performance, and has been selected as an alternate candidate in NIST PQC Round 4. However, following the key recovery attack by Castryck and Decru in 2022, SIDH was shown to be vulnerable to polynomial-time attacks on classical computers, undermining its security and removing it from consideration. Given that SIDH was regarded as the leading isogeny-based algorithm, several countermeasures, such as MSIDH, MD-SIDH, and bin/terSIDH have been proposed. However, these approaches still face performance limitations. Meanwhile, POKE, proposed by Basso and Maino at Eurocrypt 2025, is an isogeny-based public key encryption scheme that combines a SIDH-like protocol with higher-dimensional isogenies and has drawn attention for its efficient performance. In this work, we adapt POKE into a key exchange algorithm and benchmark it against M-SIDH, terSIDH, and CSIDH. Targeting NIST security level 1, POKE-based KEM is approximately 21.21 times faster than terSIDH and 64.97 times faster than CSIDH, showing that the POKE-based KEM is currently the most promising isogeny-based key exchange candidate.

</details>


### [144] [MIFA: An MILP-based Framework for Improving Differential Fault Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06837)
*Hanbeom Shin,Insung Kim,Sunyeop Kim,Byoungjin Seok,Deukjo Hong,Jaechul Sung,Seokhie Hong,Sangjin Lee,Dongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于混合整数线性规划（MILP）求解器的新型差分故障分析（DFA）框架，能够攻击更深轮次并减少所需故障注入次数。通过系统分析所有单比特翻转故障的输入差分来确定最有效的故障注入比特位置，从而进一步减少所需故障数量。对于DEFAULT密码，仅在第6轮（倒数）注入3个故障，或在第7、8轮（倒数）各注入2个故障即可将密钥空间缩减为1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DFA攻击对DEFAULT及其变体BAKSHEESH的深度和故障注入次数存在局限（如ASIACRYPT 2024的工作需5个故障攻击到倒数第5轮），需要更高效的方法实现更深轮次攻击和更少的故障数量。

Method: 提出一种基于MILP求解器的新型DFA框架，可攻击比以往更深轮次；并提出一种系统分析方法，通过分析所有可能的单比特翻转故障的输入差分，确定最有效的故障注入比特位置，从而在理论上计算所需的故障数量。

Result: 对于DEFAULT密码，在倒数第6轮注入3个故障，或在倒数第7轮和倒数第8轮各注入2个故障，即可将密钥空间缩减为1。相比此前需在倒数第5轮注入5个故障的工作，所需故障数量显著减少，攻击深度也更大。

Conclusion: 所提出的MILP-based DFA框架显著提升了DFA攻击效率，在深度和故障数量两方面均优于既有方法，且通过对故障比特位置的系统分析，实现了故障数量的理论可计算性。

Abstract: At ASIACRYPT 2021, Baksi et al. introduced DEFAULT, a block cipher designed to algorithmically resist Differential Fault Attack (DFA), claiming 64-bit DFA security regardless of the number of injected faults. At EUROCRYPT 2022, Nageler et al. demonstrated that DEFAULT's claimed DFA resistance can be broken by applying an information-combining technique. More recently, at ASIACRYPT 2024, Jana et al. improved DFA by searching for differential trails with a single solution. They showed that, for DEFAULT with a simple key schedule, injecting five faults at the fifth-to-last round reduces the key space to one, and for BAKSHEESH, injecting twelve faults at the third-to-last round achieves the same result. In this paper, we propose a new DFA framework that utilizes a Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) solver. This framework makes it possible to attack deeper rounds than previously achieved, reducing the number of fault injections required for key recovery. Furthermore, we present a method to determine the most efficient fault injection bit positions by systematically analyzing the input differences from all possible single bit-flip faults, thereby further reducing the required number of faults. This systematic analysis has the significant advantage of allowing us to theoretically calculate the required number of faults. Applying our framework, for DEFAULT, injecting three faults at the sixth-to-last round and two faults at the seventh- and eighth-to-last rounds reduces the key space to one.

</details>


### [145] [Understanding and Improving Model Editing for Secure Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06848)
*Weifeng Sun,Quanjun Zhang,Yuchen Chen,Chengran Yang,Gou Tan,David Lo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究模型编辑作为代码生成安全性的模型级加固机制，评估了3种先进的编辑方法，并提出SafeEdit——一种结合功能微调与编辑感知正则化的后编辑精炼方法，在保持安全性的同时改善功能正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在代码生成中可能复现训练数据中的不安全模式，已有研究主要聚焦于推理时加固（如CoSec），但这类方法依赖辅助组件且增加运行时开销。模型编辑作为一种模型级加固手段尚未被系统研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者在多种LLM家族上评估了3种最先进的模型编辑方法，并与推理时方法CoSec对比，考察安全性、鲁棒性、泛化性和功能正确性。针对编辑方法在未见漏洞类型上泛化不可靠且可能降低功能正确性的问题，提出SafeEdit，结合功能调优（functional tuning）与编辑感知正则化（edit-aware regularization）作为后编辑精炼步骤。

Result: 模型编辑在已知漏洞类型上的安全比率较原始模型提升15%-25%，且在提示扰动下保持稳定；但迁移到未见漏洞类型时不可靠，且可能降低功能正确性。SafeEdit在八个目标LLM上，相比UltraEdit将Pass@1在T=0.1/0.4/0.8下分别提升11.73/13.70/15.50个百分点，同时基本保持安全性；与CoSec相比，相对安全比率增益为7.54%-12.04%。CodeGuard+评估确认联合安全且正确的生成得到改善，SafeEdit与CoSec互补，组合使用可进一步兼顾安全与功能正确性。

Conclusion: 模型编辑是代码生成安全性的有效模型级加固机制，但需注意其泛化性和功能正确性权衡；SafeEdit有效缓解了这一权衡，并与推理时方法互补，为模型编辑在安全代码生成中的应用提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used for code generation, yet they can reproduce vulnerable implementations learned from insecure training patterns. Prior work has mainly explored inference-time hardening, which reduces insecure generations without modifying the target model but relies on auxiliary components and adds runtime overhead. We conduct the first systematic study of model editing as a model-level hardening mechanism for secure code generation. We evaluate 3 state-of-the-art editing methods across diverse LLM families and compare them with CoSec, a representative inference-time approach, focusing on security, robustness, generalization, and functional correctness. Model editing yields larger security gains than CoSec on seen vulnerability types, improving security ratios by 15%-25% over vanilla models, with gains remaining stable under prompt perturbations. However, these improvements transfer unreliably to unseen vulnerabilities and can reduce functional correctness. To mitigate this trade-off, we propose SafeEdit, a post-edit refinement method combining functional tuning with edit-aware regularization. Across eight target LLMs, SafeEdit improves Pass@1 over UltraEdit by 11.73/13.70/15.50 percentage points at T=0.1/0.4/0.8 while largely preserving security. Compared with CoSec, it achieves relative security-ratio gains of 7.54%-12.04%. Additional evaluation on CodeGuard+ confirms improved joint secure-and-correct generation. SafeEdit and CoSec are also complementary, and their combination can further improve security while maintaining strong functional correctness. Overall, our results provide evidence-backed guidance for applying model editing to secure code generation.

</details>


### [146] [SynChain: Inducing Computer-Use Agent Systems to Construct Their Own Attack Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06862)
*Fuyao Zhang,Jiaming Zhang,Che Wang,Boyang Chen,Yurong Hao,Xiongtao Sun,Guowei Guan,Blaise Delattre,Yang Cao,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了计算机使用智能体（CUA）面临的一种新型安全威胁：恶意影响可通过自主合成的工件（如技能和记忆条目）中的结构性冗余被隐蔽植入，从而在内部状态更新中存活并绕过标准审查机制。作者提出了SynChain攻击范式，利用持久性感知的定向监督微调诱导智能体创建看似良性但已中毒的工件，使休眠载荷能在未来工作流中作为可信上下文自动重新激活，无需任何新的恶意外部输入。在OpenClaw、Codex和Claude Code上的实验表明，SynChain在四种防御设置下均实现了高攻击成功率，证明保障CUA安全需要跨任务执行轨迹的溯源感知推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对计算机使用智能体的安全防御大多将攻击视为外部触发或时间受限的，忽视了攻击如何通过智能体自身的持久化状态进行内部传播这一关键缺口。恶意影响可以隐藏在自动合成工件中，跨越状态更新存活并绕过标准审查，需要对这一新型威胁进行系统性形式化和评估。

Method: 提出SynChain自我合成攻击范式，采用持久性感知的定向监督微调（persistence-aware directed supervised fine-tuning），诱导智能体生成表面良性但已受污染的持久化工件；同时构建了CUAChain数据集，包含30条良性任务链和三种攻击目标，用于系统评估攻击的传播与影响。

Result: 在OpenClaw、Codex和Claude Code三个系统上，在四种防御配置下进行广泛实验，SynChain展现出了高攻击成功率，并优于适应的基线方法，验证了该攻击范式的有效性与传播性。

Conclusion: 安全地保障计算机使用智能体需要超越传统外部攻击防御，引入对跨任务执行轨迹的溯源感知推理（provenance-aware reasoning），以识别和抵御经由持久化工件内部传播的隐蔽恶意影响。

Abstract: Computer-use agents~(CUAs) have transformed large language models into persistent execution systems capable of generating, storing, and reusing artifacts like skills and memory entries. However, existing security defenses largely treat attacks as externally triggered or temporally bounded, leaving a critical gap in addressing how compromise can propagate internally through an agent's own persistent state. We reveal that malicious influence can be covertly embedded into the structural redundancies of autonomously synthesized artifacts, allowing it to survive internal state updates and bypass standard vetting mechanisms. To formalize this threat, we introduce SynChain, a self-synthesized attack paradigm utilizing persistence-aware directed supervised fine-tuning to induce agents to create poisoned yet benign-looking artifacts. To systematically evaluate this propagation, we construct CUAChain, a dataset comprising 30 benign task chains and three attack objectives. SynChain enables dormant payloads to seamlessly reactivate in future workflows as trusted context, operating entirely without new malicious exogenous inputs. Extensive experiments on OpenClaw, Codex, and Claude Code under four defense settings demonstrate that SynChain achieves high attack success and outperforms adapted baselines, proving that securing CUAs requires provenance-aware reasoning over cross-task execution trajectories.

</details>


### [147] [Rigid-Covert GNSS Spoofing of UAV Swarms: A Structural Blind Spot, Its Detection Limit, and Absolute-Anchor Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06885)
*Minseok Park,Joon Soo Yoo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了无人机蜂群协同防御中相对几何校验通道的结构性盲区（刚性隐蔽平移RigidShift），该盲区使得任何仅依赖相对距离的检测器都无法观测到公共缓慢平移，并提出了锚定基准恢复管线来恢复非锚定无人机绝对位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机蜂群防御常通过交叉校验GNSS位置与无人机间测量几何来检测欺骗攻击，但作者发现相对几何通道存在原理性的盲点：公共缓慢平移不改变任意间距，因而在仅依赖相对测量时不可观测（规范自由论证）。现有惯性/GNSS监视器只能告警却无法恢复蜂群真实位置。

Method: 首先通过规范自由论证证明RigidShift对任何相对唯一检测器的不可观测性；在距离验证与半定可行性基线上验证该盲区。推导漂移相关检测下限2γ/(1-t_s/T)，并识别检测器特有噪声底。提出集中式锚定恢复管线：从内部距重建几何、以拜占庭鲁棒拟合对齐信任锚子集、恢复非锚定无人机绝对位置；引入分段估计器联合估计锚漂移、攻击率与起始时间。

Result: 在统计仿真、ArduPilot软件在环实验及Gazebo渲染视觉锚实验中，方法在约10.1m GNSS漂移下以中位误差0.39m（20种子）恢复非锚定无人机位置；渲染多SITL场景误差7.1cm（5种子）。噪声底实测斜率2.66与预测2.67一致。同时刻画了非共线锚几何、锚覆盖率、τ→0漂移-攻击混叠及多数锚被攻陷等限制。所有评估均为仿真，无RF欺骗硬件或物理蜂群。

Conclusion: 相对几何通道存在不可消除的规范自由盲区，单纯依靠内部距测量无法检测公共平移攻击；必须借助外部可信锚参考恢复可观测性。锚定恢复管线在仿真中有效，但其适用性受锚几何、覆盖率和攻击程度的明确限制，且当前评估仅在仿真层面完成，尚需实际硬件与射频欺骗场景验证。

Abstract: Cooperative UAV-swarm defenses commonly cross-check GNSS positions against measured inter-drone geometry. We show that this relative-geometry channel has a structural blind spot: a common, slowly varying translation (a rigid-covert shift, RigidShift) preserves all pairwise distances and is therefore unobservable to any relative-only detector (a gauge-freedom argument). We validate this blindness on distance-verification and semidefinite-feasibility baselines, while explicitly distinguishing it from onboard inertial/GNSS monitors that can raise a bare alarm but cannot recover the swarm's true position. To quantify when an external reference restores observability, we derive the drift-dependent detection floor $2γ/(1-t_s/T)$ for a calibrated anchor-residual detector and empirically identify an additional detector-specific noise floor (measured slope 2.66 vs. predicted 2.67). We then present a centralized anchor-rooted recovery pipeline that reconstructs swarm geometry from inter-drone ranges, aligns it to a trusted-anchor subset with Byzantine-robust fitting, and recovers the absolute positions of non-anchored drones. A segmented estimator jointly estimates anchor drift, attack rate, and onset when no clean-epoch label is available. Across statistical simulations, ArduPilot software-in-the-loop experiments, and Gazebo experiments with rendered vision anchors, the method recovers the positions of non-anchored drones to a median error of 0.39 m (20 seeds) under approximately 10.1 m of GNSS drift, and to 7.1 cm (5 seeds) in the rendered-vision multi-SITL setting. We also characterize the explicit limits imposed by non-collinear anchor geometry, anchor coverage, $τ\to0$ drift-attack aliasing, and majority anchor compromise. All evaluations are simulation-based and use no RF spoofing hardware or physical swarm.

</details>


### [148] [When Context Bites: Detecting RAG Poisoning via Document-Level Attention Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06947)
*Yingtao Ren,Ziyi Zhao,Yiwei Fu,Xiao Luo,Yu-Cheng Chang,Chin-Teng Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出D-SCAN，一种基于注意力动力学分析的检索增强生成(RAG)投毒攻击检测框架。作者发现传统基于困惑度或一致性等输出端信号的检测方法在面对精心设计的攻击时失效，因为攻击往往诱导"虚假自信"（被投毒的输出比良性输出困惑度更低）。通过分析生成器内部动态，作者识别出"注意力坍缩"(Attention Collapse)这一关键特征——受攻击的生成中注意力熵降低、集中在被投毒文档上。D-SCAN通过监控注意力动力学实现轻量级检测，在多个攻击基准上验证了有效性，且能在攻击未改变最终答案时仍检测到攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统日益广泛使用但面临投毒攻击威胁，现有的基于输出端信号（如困惑度、一致性）的检测方法在面对精心设计的攻击时会失效——攻击可诱导虚假自信，使被投毒的输出困惑度反而更低，从而绕过不确定性检测。因此需要探索生成器内部机制来寻找更可靠的攻击检测信号。

Method: 作者提出D-SCAN（Document-level Signal Collapse Analysis）框架：首先分析生成器在检索文档上的注意力分布，识别出"注意力坍缩"特征——良性生成中注意力分散，而受攻击生成中注意力熵显著降低并集中到被投毒文档上。D-SCAN通过监控这一注意力动力学信号来检测攻击，是轻量级的（仅需监控注意力而非额外的推理过程）。

Result: 在多个攻击基准上的大量实验验证了方法的有效性。D-SCAN不仅能有效检测已改变最终答案的攻击，还能在攻击未成功改变最终答案（即攻击失败但已投毒）的情况下检测到异常，表现出优于传统输出端检测方法的能力。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了传统RAG投毒攻击检测方法的根本局限，并证明生成器内部的注意力动力学可作为更鲁棒的检测信号。"注意力坍缩"现象为RAG安全防护提供了新的视角，D-SCAN作为轻量级框架兼具有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is indispensable for enhancing large language models. However, RAGs are increasingly susceptible to poisoning attacks, in which adversarial documents are injected to manipulate generator outputs. Previous methods rely on output-side signals such as perplexity and consistency checks to detect such attacks. Nevertheless, our analysis reveals that deliberate attacks often induce false confidence, where poisoned outputs exhibit even lower perplexity than benign ones, rendering uncertainty-based detection ineffective. To address this challenge, we explore the internal dynamics of the generator and identify a distinctive signature termed \textit{Attention Collapse}. Unlike the dispersed attention in benign generations, attacked generations exhibit a decrease in entropy as attention concentrates on poisoned documents. Building on these findings, we propose \texttt{D-SCAN} (Document-level Signal Collapse Analysis), a lightweight detection framework that monitors attention dynamics to identify attacked generations. Extensive experiments on multiple attack benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our method. Moreover, D-SCAN can detect attacks even when they fail to alter the final answer. Code is available at https://github.com/yingtaoren/D-Scan.git.

</details>


### [149] [Casting the Net! Revisiting MasterFace Impersonation Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06952)
*Seunghun Paik,Sunpill Kim,Chanwoo Hwang,Jae Hong Seo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明，即使仅通过合法访问公开商业API，攻击者也能利用MasterFaces攻击放大面部识别系统的冒充率，使其超越标准FMR基线。作者将MasterFaces攻击形式化为表示空间上的最大覆盖问题（称为NET），并通过利用表示空间的几何结构构建API定制的NET，在至多30次认证尝试内将多个开源和商业API面部识别系统的冒充率放大至标准FMR预期的9.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面部识别系统的冒充攻击在现实约束下（有限次决策认证尝试、无内部系统知识）大多不可行，零工冒充（基于FMR）常被视为独立基线。此前MasterFaces攻击被认为在现代FRS中无法超越FMR基线，但作者发现现实世界中许多FRS部署基于商业API构建且后端提供商公开或可推断，使得攻击者无需额外权限即可购买API服务实施攻击，这挑战了将FMR作为安全基线的假设。

Method: 作者首先观察到多个真实FRS部署使用商业API且后端服务商公开可推断，攻击者可购买按需付费的API服务。随后将MasterFaces攻击形式化为一个最大覆盖问题（称为NET），利用生物特征表示空间的几何结构来构建API定制的NET，从而在有限认证尝试次数内最大化冒充成功率。

Result: 实验表明，该攻击在至多30次认证尝试内，将多个开源和商业API面部识别系统的冒充率比标准FMR预期值放大最多9.5倍，证明了超越FMR基线的非平凡冒充攻击在现实条件下是可行的。

Conclusion: 这项工作揭示了基于商业API的FRS存在被MasterFaces攻击利用的安全漏洞，将FMR-基线的冒充攻击边界重新打开，表明现代FRS的安全性需要重新评估，单纯的FMR基线不能作为可靠的安全下限。

Abstract: Impersonation is a fundamental security threat in face recognition systems (FRSs). While the security of FRSs has been challenged by various attack vectors, under realistic adversarial capabilities, e.g., a limited number of decision-only authentication trials and no internal system knowledge, most attack techniques become infeasible. As a result, impersonation by zero-effort impostors, characterized by false match rate (FMR), is commonly regarded as a standalone baseline. A few years ago, impersonation attacks based on MasterFaces emerged as a notable security threat that could break the barrier of the FMR-based baseline under such realistic constraints. However, they were believed not to yield impersonation above the standard FMR in modern FRSs, as discussed by multiple follow-up studies. In this paper, we demonstrate that even legitimate access to public commercial APIs allows an adversary to amplify impersonation rates through MasterFaces, resulting in a non-trivial impersonation attack beyond FMR on downstream applications built on top of these APIs. We observe that several real-world FRS deployments are implemented using commercial APIs, and that the backend service provider is publicly disclosed or trivially inferable. As a result, the adversary can purchase these pay-as-you-go API services without requiring any additional privilege over the target FRS. From this observation, we formalize the MasterFaces attack as a maximum coverage problem over the biometric representation space, which we call a NET, and show that the adversary can construct an API-tailored NET by leveraging the geometric structure of the representation space. We demonstrate that our attack amplifies the impersonation rates of several open-source and commercial API-based FRSs by up to 9.5$\times$ within at most 30 authentication trials, compared to those expected from the standard FMR.

</details>


### [150] [HarnessSafe: Evaluating Safety Across Persistent Carriers in Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06984)
*Xiao Zhang,Yusheng Wang,Yuhao Fei,Dongyuan Li,Zian Liang,Liuyu Xiang,Hongxun Gu,Zhaofeng He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出HarnessSafe基准，涵盖328个可执行用例和七种持久载体家族，引入多阶段追踪式评估方法刻画攻击链在不同阶段的传播与阻断情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架通过记忆、技能、工具等持久载体跨任务保留状态，导致攻击者注入的内容可跨越系统边界并在后续良性请求中触发风险。现有基准仅覆盖少数载体或框架，且端到端攻击成功率无法反映风险的具体传播途径。

Method: 构建HarnessSafe基准，包含328个可执行用例和七种持久载体家族，每个用例以持久风险生命周期（Persistent-Risk Lifecycle）形式描述攻击影响从初始注入、跨载体与系统边界持久化、到后续良性触发和可观察违规的完整链条；并提出多阶段、基于追踪的评估方法，利用可观察的执行证据判断攻击链传播到哪一阶段及在何处被阻断。

Result: 实验表明风险遏制是载体特定的，且强烈依赖于框架-模型配置；框架和模型后端均显著影响遏制结果；攻击成功率无法反映不同的生命周期进展模式。

Conclusion: 持久载体带来的延迟安全风险需要在生命周期级粒度上进行评估，仅依靠攻击成功率不足以揭示风险传播规律，遏制效果取决于载体类型和框架-模型组合。

Abstract: Modern agent harnesses persist state across tasks and sessions through persistent carriers like memory, skills, tools, and shared artifacts. However, this capability creates delayed safety risks: attacker-influenced content can cross system boundaries and later affect the execution of a benign request. Existing benchmarks typically focus on a few carriers or harnesses, while end-to-end attack-success rates reveal little about how risks propagate. To this end, we present HarnessSafe, a benchmark comprising 328 executable cases across seven persistent-carrier families and evaluated on most mainstream agent harnesses. Each case is specified as a Persistent-Risk Lifecycle that traces attacker influence from its initial entry, through persistence across carriers and system boundaries, to a later benign trigger and an observable violation. We further introduce a multi-stage, trace-based evaluation that uses observable execution evidence to determine how far each attack chain progresses and where it is stopped. Experiments show that containment is carrier-specific and strongly depends on the harness-model configuration. Both the harness and model backend substantially shape containment outcomes, while attack success rates cannot reflect distinct lifecycle progression patterns.

</details>


### [151] [Effects of parental controls in the context of Digital Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07016)
*Selina Märchya,Mauro Vignatia,Frank Breitinger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了Microsoft、Google和Apple三家公司的家长控制系统对数字取证流程的影响，在15台Windows、Android和iOS设备上进行受控实验，评估其对证据可访问性的影响，并识别了具有取证可靠性的规避方法，为调查人员提供实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家长控制系统旨在保护未成年人上网安全，但可能无意中阻碍数字取证调查。这些系统会限制管理权限、禁用调试选项、改变数据可访问性，从而影响证据获取和分析。然而目前缺乏对这些影响的系统性实证研究。

Method: 在三家主要平台（Microsoft、Google、Apple）上，选取15台分别运行Windows、Android和iOS的设备，通过受控实验方式评估家长控制系统对取证流程的影响，包括证据可访问性评估，并测试和验证克服这些限制的取证可靠方法。

Result: 研究得出了家长控制系统对各平台取证过程的具体影响，并成功识别了在法律上可接受的、具有取证可靠性的方法来绕过或减轻这些限制，为调查人员提供了可行的取证策略。

Conclusion: 研究为受家长控制系统管辖环境下的数字取证调查提供了实践指导，有助于提升此类环境中的取证就绪性（forensic readiness），填补了该领域实证研究的空白。

Abstract: Parental control systems are designed to protect minors online, but can inadvertently obstruct digital forensic investigations. When enabled, these systems restrict administrative privileges, disable debugging options, and alter data accessibility, complicating evidence acquisition and analysis. This study empirically examines the impact of Microsoft, Google, and Apple parental controls on forensic processes across fifteen Windows, Android, and iOS devices. Through controlled experiments, we evaluate their impact on evidence accessibility and identify forensically sound methods to overcome these limitations. The findings provide practical guidance for investigators and contribute to improving forensic readiness in environments governed by parental control systems.

</details>


### [152] [Soft Redaction of Image Provenance via Zero-Knowledge Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07063)
*Muhammad Awan,John Collomosse*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出用于图像溯源（如C2PA）的软编辑（soft redaction）机制：通过零知识证明（ZKP）替换敏感的溯源声明为对隐藏数据的属性证明，支持距离证明（地理位置、生物特征嵌入、感知哈希），构建时间秒级、验证时间毫秒级，且兼容C2PA标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内容溯源标准（如C2PA）通过签名记录来源、编辑历史和权限来增强图像信任，但溯源透明性与隐私存在冲突——增强图像可信度的断言可能泄露创作者或拍摄背景的敏感信息，因此需要在保持溯源能力的同时实现隐私保护。

Method: 提出软编辑机制，用ZKPs替换敏感断言，在证明电路中利用截断Chebyshev多项式近似实现三种距离证明：(1) 位置断言支持到公共参考点的邻近证明；(2) L2距离证明扩展到生物特征嵌入，支持近似度声明以执行人格权；(3) 同一距离证明构造应用于感知哈希，支持基于水印的溯源元数据恢复中的反欺骗用例。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于ZKPs的图像溯源可提供实用的软编辑能力，证明构造可在秒级完成，验证仅需毫秒级，且与C2PA兼容。

Conclusion: 零知识证明为图像溯源中的隐私保护提供了一种可行的平衡方案，在不牺牲溯源可信度的前提下，通过隐藏敏感数据并仅证明所需属性来保护创作者隐私。

Abstract: Content provenance standards, such as C2PA, are increasingly used to attach signed records of origin, editing history, and rights to digital images. However, provenance transparency can conflict with privacy -- assertions that strengthen trust in an image may also reveal sensitive information about the creator or capture context. We propose soft redaction for image provenance: a mechanism that replaces sensitive provenance assertions with zero-knowledge proofs (ZKPs) of selected properties over hidden data. Our work focuses on distance proofs. We first show how location assertions can support proofs of proximity to a public reference point, using Chebyshev polynomial approximations within the ZKP proof circuit. We then extend the approach to L2 distance proofs over biometric embeddings, enabling privacy-preserving claims related to likeness to help enforce personality rights with images. Finally, we apply the same distance-proof construction to perceptual hashes (visual fingerprints), supporting an anti-spoofing use case in watermark-based recovery of stripped provenance metadata. Our results demonstrate that ZKPs over image provenance can provide practical soft-redaction capabilities, compatible with C2PA, that may be constructed in seconds and verified in milliseconds.

</details>


### [153] [SoK: Cryptographic Key Recovery for Cryptoasset Custody and Financial Technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07104)
*Francisco Javier Becerra Sanchez,Antonio Ken Iannillo,Radu State*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于加密资产密钥恢复的系统化知识综述（SoK），基于118篇发现文献和77篇综合文献构建了主矩阵，提出了轴优先的分类法，并识别出六个关键研究空白，为恢复感知型金融技术制定研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密资产系统将密钥控制与金融控制绑定，丢失钱包种子、托管份额、硬件设备或智能账户凭证会导致支出权限丧失，而受损的恢复机制可能引发盗窃。现有研究使用各自独立的术语（密钥备份、秘密共享、账户恢复、凭证重发、社交恢复、资产迁移）来讨论恢复问题，导致不同机制及其权衡难以横向比较。作者旨在统一这些分散的研究，建立系统化的恢复分类与分析方法。

Method: 作者进行了系统化综述研究：(1) 构建118篇论文的发现语料库，进而筛选出77篇综合语料库；(2) 在包含恢复对象、恢复语义、机制、注册与存储、授权、信任放置、故障事件、恢复后状态、验证证据、部署状态、隐私、可用性和局限性等多个维度的主矩阵中对每篇保留系统进行编码；(3) 提出轴优先分类法，将恢复系统分为秘密恢复型、混合型、控制恢复型、取证/提取型和框架导向型五类；(4) 推导通用构建模型并对照面向生产的实际设计进行验证。

Result: 研究发现恢复并非单一操作，系统可能重建原始秘密、重新生成种子、恢复共享份额、重发凭证、迁移签名权、恢复账户控制、移动资产或提取取证构件。论文识别出六大发现：恢复语义异构、恢复会转移信任、活性改进产生滥用路径、恢复后生命周期管理不均衡、协议证据多于用户证据、恢复元数据保护不足。

Conclusion: 这些研究空白为恢复感知型金融技术提出了明确的研究议程，强调需要统一恢复语义表达、审慎设计信任转移与活性机制、系统化恢复后的生命周期管理、加强用户侧证据收集，以及保护恢复元数据的安全性。

Abstract: Cryptoasset systems often bind cryptographic key control to financial control: losing a wallet seed, custody share, hardware device, or smart-account credential can remove spend authority, while compromised recovery can enable theft. Existing work treats recovery through separate vocabularies--key backup, secret sharing, account recovery, credential re-issuance, social recovery, and asset migration--making mechanisms and tradeoffs difficult to compare.
  This paper presents a Systematization of Knowledge (SoK) on cryptographic key recovery for cryptoasset custody and financial technologies. Starting from a 118-paper systematic-review discovery corpus, we derive a 77-paper synthesis corpus and code each retained system in a master matrix covering recovered objects, recovery semantics, mechanisms, enrollment and storage, authorization, trust placement, failure events, post-recovery state, validation evidence, deployment status, privacy, usability, and limitations. The matrix supports an axis-first taxonomy that separates secret-restoring, hybrid, control-restoring, forensic/extractive, and framework-oriented recovery.
  Our central observation is that recovery is not a single operation: systems may reconstruct an original secret, regenerate a seed, restore a share, reissue a credential, migrate signing authority, restore account control, move assets, or extract forensic artifacts. We derive a generalized construction model, check it against production-facing designs, and identify six findings: recovery semantics are heterogeneous; recovery shifts trust; liveness improvements create abuse paths; post-recovery lifecycle management is uneven; protocol evidence outpaces user evidence; and recovery metadata remains underprotected. These gaps motivate a research agenda for recovery-aware financial technologies.

</details>


### [154] ["Operator, can you hear me?" A Faithful Line into the UNISOC Baseband](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07143)
*Eduard Vlad,Philipp Mao,Marcel Busch,Mathias Payer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Unislop方法，通过以共享时钟锁步所有SoC外围组件（协处理器、SIM、应用处理器）的方式对基带处理器进行re-hosting，基于真实设备行为建模，使忠实度可在组件接口处验证。作者在UNISOC UDX710平台上（约占蜂窝调制解调器10-15%）成功实现代码执行、绕过固件完整性校验、仪器化基带并恢复外围环境，重宿主达到与真实设备相同的控制平面状态，建立完整PDU会话并承载真实IP流量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基带处理器随时可通过无线电访问，其最关键的安全逻辑深藏在协议状态机中（控制平面处理器：注册、认证、会话建立）。系统分析这些逻辑需要对运行中的固件进行内省，因此必须对基带进行re-hosting。现有re-hosting工作近似执行环境并且低估了基带SoC及其外围组件的复杂性，使得这一状态实际不可达。

Method: Unislop方法：不近似执行环境，而是根据真实设备的实际行为建模每个外围组件（协处理器、SIM、应用处理器），并让它们与基带在单一共享时钟上锁步运行。这使得忠实度可以在组件接口处验证而非假设。在UNISOC UDX710上，从Quectel RM500U-CNV模块出发，获得代码执行、绕过固件完整性检查、对基带进行仪器化，并从运行设备中恢复外围环境。

Result: 重宿主达到与真实设备相同的控制平面状态，建立完整PDU会话，并能在入站和出站方向承载真实IP流量。恢复的组件在UNISOC基带产品线中共享，通过额外的逆向工程工作可将设计扩展到更多目标平台。

Conclusion: Unislop证明了通过基于真实设备行为的组件建模与共享时钟锁步执行，可以在不牺牲SoC复杂度的前提下实现基带处理器的忠实re-hosting，从而打开了对这一前未系统分析的重要平台（UDX710）进行安全分析的大门，且该方法具备跨平台可扩展性。

Abstract: Baseband processors are reachable over the radio at all times. Their most security-relevant logic runs deep inside protocol state machines: the control-plane handlers that gate registration, authentication, and session setup. Analyzing that logic systematically requires introspecting the firmware as it runs, which makes re-hosting the baseband necessary. Existing re-hosting work approximates the execution environment and under-approximates the SoC complexity of the baseband processor together with its surrounding components, bringing this state practically out of reach. We instead model each surrounding component, co-processors, SIM, application processor, from what a real device does, and step them in lockstep with the baseband on one shared clock. That makes faithfulness checkable at component interfaces, rather than assumed.
  We call this method Unislop and demonstrate it on the UNISOC UDX710, a platform in an estimated 10-15% of cellular modems and in automotive systems, not systematically analyzed before. Starting from a Quectel RM500U-CNV module, we gain code execution, defeat its firmware-integrity check, instrument the baseband, and recover its peripheral environment from the running device. The resulting re-host reaches the same control-plane states as the real device, establishes a full PDU session, and carries real IP traffic on both ingress and egress. The recovered components are shared across UNISOC's baseband lineup, so with additional reverse-engineering effort the same design extends to further targets.

</details>


### [155] [An Analysis of Architectural and Operational Dynamics of Phishkits in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07451)
*Behzad Ousat,Mohammad Ali Tofighi,Estefan Schafir,Amin Kharraz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了2020至2023年间收集的1,300个现代钓鱼工具包（phishkits），从架构、源代码、通信渠道和泄露数据类型等方面进行系统分析，发现大多数phishkit功能相对简单，21.8%的kit未使用任何规避机制，且存在大量代码重用和相似实现，这为规模化检测提供了有利条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钓鱼攻击一直是攻击者青睐的载体，而phishkits是促成成功钓鱼攻击的关键要素。此前缺乏对现代phishkits的大规模系统性分析，需要深入理解其架构、规避策略和数据泄露机制，以更好地防御此类威胁。

Method: 对2020至2023年间收集的1,300个现代phishkits进行了全面的静态和动态分析，涵盖其架构设计、源代码逻辑、通信渠道（包括与远程攻击者的后门交互）以及被泄露数据的性质。特别关注了动态重定向、流量归因等规避和隐蔽机制。

Result: 识别出动态重定向和流量归因等规避/隐蔽机制；发现大量kit依赖现代消息服务进行被盗数据交换；284个（21.8%）phishkits未使用任何规避机制；虽然实现细节存在差异，但维持钓鱼页面功能的主要组件在不同kit间高度相似甚至相同；存在显著的代码复用现象。

Conclusion: 现代phishkits虽然形态多样，但核心组件和手法高度趋同，大多数kit功能水平不高且缺乏复杂规避能力。高度的代码复用和已知技巧的依赖使大量攻击具有可预测性，从而有可能在规模化检测中更有效地识别此类对抗性操作。

Abstract: Phishing attacks have always been a favored vector for adversaries to defraud users, bypass modern defense mechanisms, and penetrate critical systems. Among all the elements contributing to the creation and deployment of successful phishing attacks, phishkits stand out as a crucial parameter. Phishkits often facilitate creating and deploying compelling phishing pages, implement evasion strategies, and establish and maintain backdoors with remote adversaries for exchanging leaked data. In this work, we performed an analysis of 1,300 modern phishkits collected from 2020 to 2023. We analyzed the architecture, source code, communication channels, and the nature of leaked data shared with adversaries. We identified mechanisms for dynamic redirection and attributing incoming web traffic as part of the evasion and cloaking mechanism. We also observed heavy reliance on current messaging services for exchanging stolen data with phishers. That said, our analysis shows that the number of phishkits with advanced functionalities is quite small. We identified 284 (21.8%) phishkits that did not use any form of evasion mechanism. We also observed that while there were differences in the implementation details of phishkits, the major components that keep phishing pages functional were very similar or even identical across kits. The level of code reuse and heavy reliance on known tricks to build pre-packaged phishing pages make a large number of cases predictable, which can potentially make the detection of these adversarial operations even easier at scale.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Towards Multi-Label Graph Foundation Models: from Single-Vector Representation Learning to Multi-Semantic Basis Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06394)
*Dongxiao He,Jiayu Zhang,Jitao Zhao,Yi Wang,Di Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MSB-GFM，一个面向跨域多标签节点分类的图基础模型框架，通过多语义基元表示学习范式将每个多标签节点建模为语义基元的自适应组合，并结合语义-结构双通道架构与域对抗训练实现跨域知识迁移，解决现有图基础模型单标签假设导致的语义纠缠和跨域泛化受限问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图学习方法虽能有效建模多标签节点分类，但仅考虑同域场景，跨域泛化能力有限。图基础模型（GFMs）虽具备跨域迁移潜力，但基于单标签假设，将所有节点嵌入为单一表示，对多标签节点而言该表示用单个点近似多个语义，导致语义纠缠，难以同时判别多个标签。

Method: 1) 提出多语义基元表示学习范式，将每个多标签节点建模为语义基元的自适应组合，增强多语义建模的表示能力；2) 设计语义-结构双通道架构，结合域对抗训练实现有效的跨域知识迁移。

Result: 大量实验证明MSB-GFM在跨域多标签节点分类任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: MSB-GFM通过多语义基元表示解决了多标签节点的语义纠缠问题，并通过双通道架构和域对抗训练实现了跨域泛化，为图基础模型在多标签场景下的应用提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Multi-label node classification is an important yet challenging task in graph learning, where nodes exhibit multiple semantics simultaneously. Existing methods for multi-label node classification can effectively model multiple labels, while only considering in-domain scenarios where the model needs to be trained and tested within the same graph domain, resulting in limited cross-domain generalization. Recently, Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for learning transferable graph representations across diverse graph domains and downstream tasks. However, existing GFMs are built upon single-label assumption, where all nodes are arbitrarily regarded as containing only one class of semantic and embedded into a single representation. For multi-label nodes, such a representation essentially approximates multiple semantics with a single point in the representation space, inevitably leading to semantic entanglement and making simultaneous discrimination of multiple labels difficult. To address these limitations, we propose a Multi-Semantic Basis Graph Foundation Model (MSB-GFM), a framework for cross-domain multi-label node classification. Specifically, we introduce a multi-semantic basis representation learning paradigm that models each multi-label node as an adaptive composition of semantic bases, thereby enabling flexible representational capacity for modeling multiple semantics. Furthermore, we develop a semantic-structure dual-channel architecture with domain adversarial training for effective cross-domain knowledge transfer. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our model.

</details>


### [157] [EntropyMoE: Entropy-Aware Sparse Expert Routing for Tokenizer-Free LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06398)
*Bo Liu,Muxuab Yu,Yu Zhang,Pengfei Gao,Yongping Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EntropyMoE是一种专为动态字节补丁设计的混合专家（MoE）架构，利用补丁熵作为路由坐标来选择专家，取代了传统密集前馈计算，在最低持出每字节比特数上优于匹配的密集和稀疏基线，同时保持相当的下游准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有字节级大语言模型（LLM）通过将字节分组为动态大小的补丁使无分词器建模更具竞争力，但现有字节补丁架构对所有补丁应用相同的密集前馈计算，无法根据补丁语义和粒度的变化自适应调整模型容量。这种统一计算方式限制了模型对复杂补丁的处理能力。

Method: EntropyMoE用Top-K专家层替换全局补丁Transformer中的密集前馈模块。每个动态补丁是专家路由的基本单元，其字节覆盖范围决定工作负载计数的贡献。路由器直接根据补丁熵选择专家——熵是动态补丁构建背后的粒度信号，被用来组织稀疏计算。补丁熵和长度共同定义特征空间以调节专家特化。

Result: 实验表明EntropyMoE在匹配的密集和稀疏基线中取得最低的持出每字节比特数（held-out bits-per-byte），同时保持可比较的下游任务准确率。这验证了补丁熵作为稀疏条件计算路由坐标的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将MoE建模扩展到了基于分词器的表征之外，证明补丁熵是组织稀疏计算的有效路由坐标，为无分词器大语言模型提供了可扩展的稀疏化方案。

Abstract: Recent byte-level large language models (LLMs) have made tokenizer-free modeling increasingly competitive by grouping bytes into dynamically sized patches. However, existing byte-patch architectures still apply the same dense feed-forward computation to every patch. This uniform computation cannot adapt model capacity to variations in patch semantics and granularity. We address this limitation with EntropyMoE, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture designed for dynamic byte patches. EntropyMoE replaces the dense feed-forward modules in the global patch Transformer with Top-K expert layers. Each dynamic patch serves as the basic unit of expert routing, and its byte coverage determines its contribution to workload accounting. The router selects experts directly from patch entropy, using the same granularity signal that underlies dynamic patch construction to organize sparse computation. Patch entropy and length jointly define the feature space for regulating expert specialization. Experiments show that EntropyMoE achieves the lowest held-out bits-per-byte among matched dense and sparse baselines while maintaining comparable downstream accuracy. These results establish patch entropy as an effective routing coordinate for sparse conditional computation and extend Mixture-of-Experts modeling beyond tokenizer-based representations.

</details>


### [158] [Beyond Routing Weights: Faithful Response-Level Interpretation of Mixture-of-Experts Reward Models via Contribution Contrast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06400)
*Yifan Wang,Jinyi Mu,Mayank Jobanputra,Yu Wang,Soyoung Oh,Isabel Valera,Vera Demberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo（Contribution-Contrast）方法，用于解释稀疏MoE奖励模型的行为。与仅基于路由权重的方法不同，CoCo通过选择具有最大贡献差异的chosen-rejected响应对来联合捕捉专家的路由和偏好行为，从而更忠实、更连贯地表征专家的角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励模型在从人类偏好中学习中至关重要，但识别其预测驱动因素仍具挑战性。现有稀疏MoE奖励模型通过路由权重将提示路由到专门专家来提升可解释性，但路由权重仅揭示专家"接收"了哪些提示，而非它如何"评判"响应，对专家行为的刻画不完整。

Method: 提出Contribution-Contrast（CoCo）响应级解释方法。该方法利用具有最大贡献差异的chosen-rejected响应对来刻画专家角色，同时捕捉路由行为和偏好行为（即专家对候选响应的评判逻辑），通过贡献对比联合建模专家如何接收和如何评判。

Result: 在自动评估和人工评估中，CoCo生成的解释比基于路由、基于分数和基于稀疏自编码器的替代方法更连贯、更忠实、更专门化，同时保持了具有竞争力的奖励建模准确性。

Conclusion: 这是首个对MoE奖励模型解释方法的系统性研究，CoCo通过贡献对比的方式为MoE奖励模型提供了更全面的行为理解视角。

Abstract: Reward models are central to learning from human preferences, yet identifying what drives their predictions remains challenging. Recent sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) reward models seek to improve interpretability by routing prompts to specialized experts and characterizing experts through examples with high routing weights. However, routing weights only reveal which prompts an expert $\textit{receives}$, not how it $\textit{judges}$ responses, providing only a partial account of expert behavior. We therefore propose $\textbf{Co}$ntribution-$\textbf{Co}$ntrast ($\textbf{CoCo}$) response-level interpretation, which faithfully characterizes experts' roles using chosen-rejected response pairs with the largest contribution contrasts, jointly capturing routing and preference behavior. Across automatic and human evaluations, CoCo yields more coherent, faithful, and specialized interpretations than router-based, score-based, and sparse autoencoder-based alternatives while maintaining competitive reward modeling accuracy. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of interpretation methods for MoE reward models.

</details>


### [159] [Interpretable Unsupervised Community Detection with LLM-Symbolized Structured Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06402)
*Aoting Zeng,Kai Wang,Jianwei Wang,Yuxiang Sun,Yizhang He,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LUCID是一种基于大语言模型（LLM）引导的社区检测方法，无需训练和标注数据即可实现可解释的社区发现。该方法受自然系统中相变动力学启发，设计为四阶段流水线（初始化、合并、精炼、选择），通过LLM诱导的显式逻辑规则完成社区检测，在真实数据集上达到最先进的无监督性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典目标驱动的社区检测方法在处理复杂图结构时表现不佳；深度学习方法虽提升性能但牺牲了可解释性，且依赖标签数据和训练过程。LLM具备强大的推理能力和世界知识，有望实现可解释、无需标注的社区检测，但尚未被有效利用。

Method: LUCID采用四阶段流水线：(1) 局部视图社区初始化阶段，利用k-ego上下文和无监督节点角色编码局部图结构；(2) 多因素社区合并阶段，使用LLM诱导的规则迭代合并局部社区；(3) 多粒度社区精炼阶段，并行应用LLM诱导的从粗到细规则以减少边界噪声；(4) 全局视图社区选择阶段，基于拓扑紧密度和边界清晰度识别高质量社区。整个流程中，LLM将隐性知识转化为显式、可解释的逻辑规则。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的广泛实验表明，LUCID作为一种无监督方法达到了最先进的性能，并持续优于领先的无监督和半监督基线方法。

Conclusion: LUCID成功利用LLM的推理能力实现了可解释、无需训练和标注的社区检测，证明了LLM引导的方法在无监督图分析任务中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Community detection is a fundamental task in graph analytics that aims to identify cohesive groups of entities with similar behaviors or interests. Classic objective-driven methods struggle with complex graph structures, while deep-learning approaches improve performance at the expense of interpretability and rely on labeled data and training. Large language models (LLMs), with strong reasoning capabilities and world knowledge, are promising for interpretable, label-free community detection. To leverage these strengths, we propose LUCID, an LLM-guided, interpretable, training-free, and unsupervised community detection method. Inspired by phase-transition kinetics in natural systems, where complex structures emerge through initialization, merging, refinement, and selection, LUCID is designed as a four-stage pipeline. Within this pipeline, the LLM induces formal rules that translate implicit knowledge into explicit and interpretable logical structures. Specifically, (1) the Local-View Community Initialization stage encodes local graph structures using k-ego contexts and unsupervised node roles; (2) the Multi-factor Community Merge stage uses LLM-induced rules to iteratively merge local communities; (3) the Multi-grain Community Refinement stage applies LLM-induced coarse-to-fine rules in parallel to reduce boundary noise; and (4) the Global-view Community Selection stage identifies high-quality communities based on topological compactness and boundary clarity. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that LUCID, as an unsupervised approach, achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently outperforms leading unsupervised and semi-supervised baselines.

</details>


### [160] [ADIAS: Automated Design of Interactive Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06410)
*Lekang Jiang,Bohan Tang,Stephan Goetz,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出以"问题为中心"（issue-centric）的自动化智能体优化框架ADIAS，通过持久化问题状态显式地跨轮次传递修复进度，替代传统的以候选为中心（candidate-centric）的方法。在五个交互式基准上，ADIAS平均超越最强基线25.2%，消融实验显示移除持久问题状态或替换为候选中心策略会导致最高40.7%的性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化智能体设计方法大多以候选为中心组织跨轮次经验，导致修复进度隐式化，存在修复目标定位低效、部分进展整合缓慢、无效干预跨轮次传播等问题。作者认为需要将修复进度作为显式的持久化问题状态来引导优化过程。

Method: 作者提出issue-centric的自动化智能体优化形式化方法，并实例化为ADIAS框架，包含两大机制：(1) 持久化问题状态（persistent issue state），维护稳定的问题身份、生命周期状态、支持证据和干预-结果历史；(2) 问题引导优化（issue-guided optimization），利用该状态联合提出修复目标和修订方向，进行聚焦的全代码修改。

Result: 在五个交互式基准上，ADIAS比最强基线平均提升25.2%，在四个骨干模型上均取得一致增益。消融实验表明，移除持久问题状态或改用候选中心策略会导致最高40.7%的性能下降。

Conclusion: 以问题为中心的优化范式通过显式持久化修复进度，有效克服了候选中心方法的效率瓶颈，显著提升了自动化智能体设计的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Automated agent design improves agent harnesses through iterative revision, evaluation, and feedback summarization. Existing methods are largely candidate-centric: cross-round experience is organized around candidate agents, which leaves the repair progress implicit. This causes inefficient repair targeting, slow consolidation of partial progress, and propagation of ineffective interventions across rounds. Therefore, we formulate issue-centric agent optimization, in which repair progress is carried forward as an explicit persistent issue state to guide optimization, rather than re-derived from candidate history in each round. We instantiate the formulation in ADIAS, a framework for automated full-code agent design with two mechanisms. A persistent issue state maintains stable issue identities, lifecycle status, supporting evidence, and intervention-outcome histories. Issue-guided optimization uses this state to jointly propose repair targets and revision directions for subsequent focused full-code modification. Across five interactive benchmarks, ADIAS outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.2% on average and achieves consistent gains across four backbone models. Controlled ablations further show that removing persistent issue state or replacing issue-centric revision with candidate-centric policies leads to performance drops of up to 40.7%.

</details>


### [161] [Learning to Predict Middle-Layer Attention in MLLMs for Visual Token Prunin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06411)
*Yuyao Sun,Tao Deng,Shuang Li,Deqing Wang,Hao Geng,Minjun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAP提出一种中间层注意力预测方法，通过问题对比教师选择识别样本特定的教师层，并将其注意力蒸馏到轻量级预测器中，在首个语言模型层之前完成视觉token修剪，仅保留5.56%的视觉token即可保持97.5%的性能，实现3.09倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在处理大量视觉token时计算成本高昂。现有基于固定中间层注意力引导的视觉token修剪方法存在两个问题：一是固定中间层的注意力对问题的响应程度在不同样本间差异显著，导致选择次优；二是获取合适中间层的注意力需要先让视觉token通过多个语言模型层，此时已消耗大量计算。

Method: 提出Middle-layer Attention Prediction (MAP)方法，包含两个核心组件：1）Question Contrastive Teacher Selection：通过对比原始问题与参考问题下的注意力，为每个样本选择特定的教师层；2）将所选教师层的注意力蒸馏到轻量级预测器中，该预测器从多模态输入特征估计视觉token重要性。推理时将预测的重要性分数与多样性准则结合，在首个语言模型层之前完成token修剪。

Result: 在LLaVA-NeXT-7B上跨10个基准测试，MAP仅保留5.56%的视觉token即保持未修剪模型97.5%的性能，实现3.09倍的端到端加速，并且与现有推理加速技术兼容。

Conclusion: MAP通过样本特定的教师层选择和轻量级注意力预测，解决了固定中间层修剪的次优性和开销问题，实现了高效的视觉token修剪，无需推理时计算注意力图即可保持接近原始性能。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance across diverse vision-language tasks, but their efficiency is limited by the cost of processing numerous visual tokens. Visual token pruning can reduce this cost, but requires accurate token importance estimates. Recent studies have demonstrated that text-to-vision attention from middle language model layers can effectively guide visual token pruning, typically using attention from a predefined middle layer to select the visual tokens to retain. Two problems therefore remain. First, our analysis shows that the layer whose attention is most responsive to the question varies substantially across samples, making a fixed layer suboptimal. Second, obtaining attention from the appropriate middle layer requires processing numerous visual tokens through several language model layers, by which point considerable computation has already been spent. To address both problems, we propose Middle-layer Attention Prediction (MAP), which uses Question Contrastive Teacher Selection to identify a sample-specific teacher layer by contrasting attention under the original and reference questions, and distills attention from the selected layer into a lightweight predictor that estimates visual token importance from multi-modal input features. During inference, MAP combines the predicted importance scores with a diversity criterion to prune visual tokens before the first language model layer. Thus, MAP requires no attention maps for pruning and remains compatible with existing inference acceleration techniques. Across ten benchmarks on LLaVA-NeXT-7B, MAP retains 97.5% of the unpruned model performance with only 5.56% of the visual tokens, yielding a 3.09x end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [162] [WebGrader: Training LLMs for Web Development with Self-Evolving Programmatic Grader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06474)
*Boshui Chen,Huiping Liu,Shaolei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了WebGrader，一个自进化的程序化奖励评估器，用于大语言模型生成完整网站的场景。它从网站请求中自动推导交互流程，将其表示为可执行的Flow Contract，并以执行结果作为RL奖励，解决了手工浏览脚本成本高和VLM/GUI-agent评分器可能过早判定胜负的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型从自然语言描述生成完整网站时，强化学习是缩小功能差距的核心手段，但其训练受限于奖励设计：手工编写的浏览器脚本虽然可执行但编写成本高，尤其在开放需求下；而基于VLM和GUI-agent的评分器虽然可扩展，但可能在关键状态出现前就下结论，导致不准确的奖励信号。

Method: WebGrader包含四个核心模块：（1）测试规划：从每个网站请求中自主推导所需的交互流程；（2）动作接地：将目标动作与源代码和实时DOM进行对应，在真实浏览器中实现生成的项目；（3）证据收集：沿同一浏览器轨迹收集视觉、DOM、响应和持久状态证据；（4）语义判断：只有当观察到请求的状态转换后才给出Pass判定。此外，残差驱动的离线循环发现可复用的验证技能，在不相交的验证页面上筛选，并在策略训练前冻结晋升的技能图。

Result: 在WebGen-Bench上，WebGrader训练的8B策略达到了52.01%的功能成功率，比匹配的外观加脚本奖励高出7.88个百分点，并超过了o4-mini和DeepSeek-v4-flash。在WG-core-250上，策略达到44.953的满分，超过了Qwen3-Coder-480B。

Conclusion: WebGrader通过分离测试规划、动作接地、证据收集和语义判断四个阶段，确保仅在观察到请求的状态转换后才发出Pass判定。实验结果表明，这种自进化的程序化评估方法显著优于手工脚本和VLM/GUI-agent评分方法，有效提升了策略的功能成功率和整体性能。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate complete websites from natural-language descriptions, and reinforcement learning has become a central approach to closing their remaining functional gap. This training regime is bottlenecked by reward design. Hand-authored browser scripts are executable yet costly to write for open-ended requirements, while VLM and GUI-agent graders scale but may issue verdicts before observing the decisive state. We propose WebGrader, a self-evolving programmatic grader that autonomously derives the required interaction flows from each website request, represents each flow as an executable Flow Contract, and uses its execution outcome as an RL reward. WebGrader materializes the generated project in a live browser, grounds target actions against the source code and live DOM, and collects visual, DOM, response, and persistent-state evidence along the same browser trajectory. A residual-driven offline loop then discovers reusable verifier skills, screens them on disjoint validation pages, and freezes the promoted skill graph before policy training. By separating test planning, action grounding, evidence collection, and semantic judgment, WebGrader issues a Pass verdict only after observing the requested transition. On WebGen-Bench, WebGrader trains an 8B policy to a 52.01% functional success rate, outperforming a matched appearance-plus-script reward by 7.88 points and surpassing o4-mini and DeepSeek-v4-flash. On WG-core-250, the policy reaches a Full Score of 44.953 and surpasses Qwen3-Coder-480B.

</details>


### [163] [Can MLLMs Decode the Creative Leap? Introducing C4 for Cross-Concept Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06501)
*Ming Wang,Yuqing Zhang,Tingna Xie,Xiangju Li,Xiaocui Yang,Daling Wang,Shi Feng,Yifei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出C4，一个基于认知科学启发的成语跨概念创造力评估框架，通过将项目建构（编码）和模型推理（解码）相对应，构建了包含184个合成项目和37个人类创作项目的C4-Eval数据集，在十种MLLM上评估发现最强闭源模型仅达约50%准确率，揭示了当前多模态大模型在跨概念创造性解码方面的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的创造性能力在设计与沟通等领域至关重要，但因其缺乏明确目标和奖励信号而难以评估。跨概念理解是接受型创造力的核心认知能力，当前缺乏系统化的评估框架来衡量MLLM通过非明显但有意义的概念关系恢复预期含义的能力。

Method: 将项目建构操作化为跨概念编码，模型推理操作化为跨概念解码。构建C4框架，其编码组件通过人工标注并第三方审查的跨概念网络，将目标槽映射到可成像的替代概念，沿桥路径生成，难度由桥数量和深度索引，具有明确结构和精确答案。基于此构建C4-Eval数据集（184个合成+37个人类创作成语），每个项目在五种任务设置下实例化，共884个主要答案恢复案例。

Result: 在十种评估的MLLM上，最强闭源模型达到50.7%和48.0%的主要准确率，开源模型显著更低。候选项约束能大幅提升准确率，但桥提示和解释请求仅带来适度提升。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM在通过跨概念关系解码创造性编码含义方面存在实质性差距，C4框架为评估和推动MLLM的接受型创造力提供了结构化、可复现的基准。

Abstract: Creative capabilities of MLLMs matter in design, communication, education, and human--AI collaboration, yet remain difficult to evaluate because explicit targets and reward signals are scarce compared with accuracy-oriented tasks. Cross-concept understanding is a core cognitive capacity underlying receptive creativity. It enables a perceiver to recover intended meaning from non-obvious but meaningful conceptual relations. We operationalize item construction as cross-concept encoding and model inference as cross-concept decoding. We introduce C4, a cognition-inspired evaluation framework for Chengyu (Chinese idiom)-based Cross-Concept Creativity. Its encoding component maps target slots to imageable substitute concepts along bridge paths in a manually annotated and third-party-reviewed cross-concept network, enabling batch generation with explicit structure, difficulty indexed by bridge count and depth, and exact answers.
