Result: 该方法在高保真仿真中的不平地形场景以及68 kg四足机器人的真实平地实验中均得到验证，证明其在实际执行器功率损失情况下能够有效维持稳定性和机动性。

Conclusion: 通过非对称actor-critic结合潜对齐损失与可学习步态频率的方法，能够在执行器功率损失时实现自适应步态重组织和时序调整，适用于大型四足机器人的容错运动控制，为硬件故障下的鲁棒运动提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Hardware failures require legged robots to rapidly reorganize coordination and gait timing to maintain stability and mobility. This is particularly challenging for larger quadrupeds, where increased mass and tighter actuation limits reduce the feasibility of aggressive, high-frequency compensation strategies often observed on smaller platforms. In this work, we propose a deep reinforcement learning approach for fault-tolerant locomotion under actuator power loss. The method employs an asymmetric actor-critic architecture in which the critic has access to privileged information during training, while the actor learns to reconstruct a corresponding latent representation from proprioceptive observations. We introduce a latent-alignment loss that encourages consistency between actor and critic representations. Additionally, we augment the action space with a learnable gait frequency parameter, enabling adaptive gait timing in response to terrain variations and actuator degradation without predefined faulty-leg strategies. The approach is validated in high-fidelity simulation on uneven terrain and real-world experiments on flat ground using a 68 kg quadruped robot.

</details>


### [361] [Depth-Wise Probing and Pruning of the Planning Token in a Driving Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07361)
*Harisankar Babu,Benjamin Coors,Christopher Lang,Hendrik Berkemeyer,Tamim Asfour,Simon Foell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）驾驶模型的解码器层进行诊断分析，发现语义意图可在浅层（第一层即达97.7%准确性）线性解码，而轨迹兼容性需在深层逐步提升。通过基于角度偏差的层排序，可移除32层中的8层，仅造成约5%的开放环路误差增加和1.33倍解码器加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型通过深层语言模型路由驾驶决策，但动作本身是否真的需要如此深的网络深度尚不明确。作者希望探究VLA模型的解码器深度冗余程度，理解规划信息在解码器层间的表示和分布方式，从而为模型压缩和高效推理提供依据。

Method: 选取一个代表性驾驶VLA模型（ORION），利用规划token作为轨迹空间logit透镜，从全部32个解码器层分别解码规划token，测量两个信号：（1）导航命令的线性可解码性（command-probe accuracy）；（2）与冻结原生规划器的轨迹兼容性（open-loop Avg-L2）。同时通过从第一层学习读取头（learned readouts）验证规划信息是否已在早期层存在，并依据解码器层引起的规划token角偏差进行层排序并剪枝。

Result: 命令探针准确性在第一层解码器后即达97.7%（随机基线16.7%），表明语义意图在浅层就可线性解码；而兼容性逐步提升，开放环路Avg-L2仅在最终层达到最小值2.11米。第一层的学习读取头可恢复大部分兼容性差距，说明规划信息已存在于早期层但未以部署规划器所期望的格式呈现。基于角度偏差排序剪枝8/32层后，相对开放环路误差仅增加约5%，并实现1.33倍解码器加速。

Conclusion: VLA模型中存在显著的解码器深度冗余：语义意图在浅层即可提取，规划信息早期已经存在但格式不匹配。通过合理的层剪枝策略可以在保持性能的同时实现加速。该发现受限于ORION检查点和Bench2Drive测试基准，且在该样本量下未检测到统计上显著的家族特定退化。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models route driving decisions through a deep language model, but it is unclear how much of that depth the action itself requires. We study a representative driving VLA whose entire plan is carried by a single planning token that a generative planner decodes into a trajectory. Borrowing the planner as a trajectory-space logit lens, we decode the planning token from every one of the 32 decoder layers and measure two signals: the linear decodability of the navigation command and trajectory compatibility with the frozen native planner. Our diagnostic shows that semantic intent is linearly decodable early: command-probe accuracy reaches 97.7\% after the first decoder layer, compared with 16.7\% chance. In contrast, compatibility with the frozen native planner improves gradually across depth, with open-loop Avg-L2 reaching its minimum of 2.11\,m only at the final layer. Learned readouts from the first layer recover much of this gap, indicating that planning information is already present early but is not yet represented in the format expected by the deployed planner. Ranking decoder layers by the angular deviation they induce in the planning token permits removal of 8 of 32 layers within an approximately 5\% relative open-loop error increase and yields a measured 1.33$\times$ decoder speedup. At the evaluated sample size, no family-specific degradation is statistically resolved. These findings are limited to the evaluated ORION checkpoint and Bench2Drive setup.

</details>


### [362] [TRACE: Ergodic Trajectory Optimization for Active Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02304)
*Ziyue Zheng,Linli Shi,Bingkun He,Wen Jiang,Ziyun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE，将主动重建（active reconstruction）问题重新建模为遍历覆盖（ergodic coverage）问题，使传感器轨迹的时间平均空间统计量与当前地图诱导的目标信息分布相匹配，取代了传统的贪心next-best-view策略。该方法通过核遍历水平规划器（kernel-ergodic horizon planner）在线推导目标分布，结合梯度流和足迹消耗机制，在Replica数据集上相比NBV基线PSNR提升1.5 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯溅射（Gaussian-splatting）地图的主动重建系统采用贪心策略，每步仅优化单个next-best-view（NBV），并通过短视距路径规划连接所选视图。这种贪心解耦方式忽略了场景信息的全局结构，导致轨迹低效，在视图间移动时浪费大量感知能力。

Method: 将主动重建建模为遍历覆盖问题：要求传感器轨迹的时间平均空间统计量与目标信息分布相匹配。目标分布由不确定性（uncertainty）和可见性（visibility）在线推导，由当前地图诱发。随后通过具有梯度流（gradient flow）和足迹消耗（footprint depletion）机制的核遍历水平规划器（kernel-ergodic horizon planner）计算遍历轨迹，从而实现建图与轨迹优化之间的闭环。

Result: 在Replica数据集上与Next-Best-View（NBV）基线进行对比，TRACE实现了1.5 dB的PSNR提升。

Conclusion: 通过将主动重建从贪心单视图优化提升为全局遍历覆盖问题，TRACE证明了考虑轨迹时空统计与信息分布匹配的有效性，为主动重建提供了一种更具全局意识的新范式。

Abstract: Existing active reconstruction systems with Gaussian-splatting maps select observations greedily, optimizing a single next-best-view (NBV) at each step and connecting the chosen views by short-horizon path planning. This greedy decoupling disregards the global structure of scene information, producing inefficient trajectories that waste sensing capacity in transit between selected views. In this work, we study active reconstruction as an ergodic coverage problem: the time-averaged spatial statistics of the sensor trajectory should match a target information distribution induced by the current map. Our approach derives this target distribution online from uncertainty and visibility, and calculates ergodic trajectories via a kernel-ergodic horizon planner with gradient flow and footprint depletion, closing the loop between mapping and trajectory optimization. We thoroughly evaluate TRACE on the Replica dataset against the Next-Best-View (NBV) baselines, improving PSNR by 1.5 dB. Code: https://github.com/spikelab-jhu/trace-active-reconstruction.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [363] [Rendezvous of Mobile Deterministic Automata in Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06482)
*Bibhuti Das,Andrzej Pelc*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究两个移动代理（确定性有限自动机）在无标注节点图中相遇（rendezvous）的问题，探讨是否存在RV-universal DFA。研究发现：无标记能力时连线图都没有通用DFA；使用有限数量的静止卵石在树上仍不存在RV-universal DFA；但每个代理配备一个可移动卵石时，存在针对树的RV-universal DFA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在多代理系统中，无需全局信息、仅通过有限的本地能力（如确定性有限自动机）实现确定性相遇的可行性边界，特别是识别何种资源（卵石的可移动性）在何种图类（树）上足以实现通用解决方案。

Method: 首先通过反例证明无标记下即使线图也没有RV-universal DFA；接着分别针对静止卵石和可移动卵石两种情况进行分析，通过构造性证明（设计通用DFA）和不可能性证明（反例构造）来刻画问题边界。

Result: (1) 无标记能力时，线图类中不存在RV-universal DFA；(2) 即使使用任意有限数量的可移动卵石，所有图实例类中也不存在RV-universal DFA；(3) 对于任意有限数量的静止卵石，树类中不存在RV-universal DFA；(4) 每个代理配备单个可移动卵石时，存在针对树的RV-universal DFA。

Conclusion: 卵石的可移动性是实现树上通用相遇的关键特征：静止卵石即使数量再多也不足以实现通用相遇，而单个可移动卵石即可实现。

Abstract: Two mobile agents, modeled as identical deterministic finite automata (DFA) navigating in synchronous rounds in a graph with unlabeled nodes, have to meet at some node. The well-researched task of meeting in a graph is known as rendezvous. Agents start at adversarially chosen distinct nodes in possibly different rounds. An instance of the rendezvous problem is the underlying graph, together with the initial nodes $u$ and $v$ of the agents. Such an instance is feasible, if there exists a DFA (possibly working only for this instance), such that its identical copies starting at nodes $u$ and $v$, with an arbitrary delay, accomplish rendezvous. A DFA is RV-universal for a class of instances, if it guarantees rendezvous of its copies starting at the designated nodes with arbitrary delay, for all feasible instances of this class. Our goal is to investigate the existence of RV-universal DFA.
  We start by observing that if agents cannot mark nodes in any way then there does not exist a RV-universal DFA even for the class of instances where the underlying graph is a line. Hence we allow the use of identical pebbles to mark the nodes by the agents. We consider stationary pebbles that can be dropped by agents at nodes but cannot be picked up, and movable pebbles that can be dropped by agents and later picked up. We observe that, even in the more powerful scenario of movable pebbles, if agents are equipped with any finite number of pebbles, there is no RV-universal DFA for the class of all instances. Hence we restrict attention to instances where the underlying graph is a tree. Our main contribution are two contrasting results showing that movability of pebbles is a crucial feature. We first prove that for any finite number of stationary pebbles there is no RV-universal DFA for trees, and then we design a RV-universal DFA for trees, where each agent is equipped with a single movable pebble.

</details>


### [364] [Cascade: Exploiting SLO-Aware latency budget for fair and high goodput LLM inference serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06557)
*Muhammad Adnan,Rohan Mahapatra,Prashant J. Nair,Daniel Berger,Pantea Zardoshti,Rodrigo Fonseca,Esha Choukse*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Cascade是一个LLM服务系统，通过定义每个请求的"延迟预算"（即请求的SLO与其预测剩余服务时间之间的差额），并利用这一单一预算联合协调请求调度和KV缓存管理，从而在异构请求场景下显著提升SLO满足率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务平台用统一的响应延迟SLO来约束所有请求，但同一服务内的不同请求在输入长度、生成长度、执行成本和可复用KV缓存状态上差异可达数量级。因此，受同一SLO约束的请求具有不同的紧迫性：有些有大量延迟余量，有些几乎没有。现有SLO感知调度器仅用deadline来管理请求排序，忽略了KV缓存管理层面的优化机会。

Method: Cascade为每个请求定义一个延迟预算（SLO与预测剩余服务时间的差值），并基于请求特征、KV缓存状态和当前系统负载持续更新该预算。它使用这一单一预算联合协调两个层面：(1) 调度器优先处理剩余预算很少的请求；(2) 内存管理器根据预算决定非驻留KV状态是从深层存储恢复/预取、保留在HBM中，还是重新计算。

Result: 在三个大语言模型的生产级trace上，与vLLM默认的FCFS调度器相比，Cascade将goodput提升高达2.4倍，并将SLO违规率降低40%。

Conclusion: 通过将队列延迟和数据移动开销定向分配到能够吸收这些开销的请求上，Cascade在异构请求类别之间保持公平性的同时，显著提升了SLO满意的goodput。

Abstract: The reasoning and agentic capabilities of large language models have expanded the range of applications they support, from short interactive exchanges to long, compute-heavy requests. LLM serving platforms today define response-latency service-level objectives, even though requests within the same service can differ by orders of magnitude in input length, generation length, execution cost, and the availability of reusable KV-cache state. As a result, requests governed by the same service level objective have different urgency: after accounting for the time required to execute them, some have substantial latency headroom while others have almost none. We define this headroom---the difference between a request's service level objective and its predicted remaining service time---as its per-request latency budget.
  We present Cascade, an LLM serving system that estimates and continuously updates this budget from request characteristics, KV-cache state, and current system load. Unlike prior SLO-aware schedulers that use deadlines to govern request ordering alone, Cascade uses a single per-request budget to jointly coordinate request scheduling and KV-cache management across the memory hierarchy. Its scheduler prioritizes requests with little remaining budget, while its memory manager uses the same budget to decide whether non-resident KV state should be restored or prefetched from a deeper tier, retained in HBM, or recomputed. By directing queueing and data-movement overhead toward requests that can absorb it, Cascade improves SLO-satisfied goodput while preserving fairness across heterogeneous request classes. On production traces across three large language models, Cascade improves goodput by up to2.4x and reduces SLO violations by 40% relative to the default vLLM first-come, first-served scheduler.

</details>


### [365] [MARS: A Monte Carlo Tree Search-based Adaptive and Responsive Scheduler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06629)
*Yash Kurkure,Yihe Zhang,Zhiling Lan,Michael E. Papka*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MARS是一个基于蒙特卡洛树搜索的训练自由的HPC调度器，通过可配置的奖励函数实现优化目标，在Argonne两个生产系统的年度工作负载上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HPC调度依赖静态启发式方法和人工管理，而DRL方法虽然性能好但需要历史训练数据且优化目标固定，每次优先级变化都需要重新训练，缺乏灵活性和适应性。

Method: MARS采用训练自由的蒙特卡洛树搜索框架，结合轻量级离散事件模拟器在严格时间预算内探索调度决策的未来后果，通过可配置的奖励函数而非固定学习模型来适应不同的优化目标（如等待时间最小化或利用率最大化）。

Result: 在Argonne领导计算设施的Theta（4360节点）和Polaris（560节点）系统上，面对一年生产工作负载，MARS-CW在Theta上减少尾部等待时间64%，在Polaris上减少43%；MARS-CU在维护前48小时恢复了系统利用率，证明通过奖励重配置可以灵活切换优化目标。

Conclusion: MARS通过奖励函数的可配置性和前瞻性调度能力，同时优于DRL和传统启发式方法，无需训练即可适应不同优化目标，为HPC调度提供了更灵活高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern High Performance Computing systems depend on static heuristics and manual administration for job scheduling and reservation management. Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) has shown promising scheduling performance but requires historical training data and fixes the optimization goal at training time, forcing operators to retrain whenever priorities shift. We introduce MARS (Monte Carlo Tree Search-based Adaptive and Responsive Scheduler), a training-free HPC scheduler whose optimization goal is configurable through a reward function rather than baked into a learned model. MARS uses a lightweight discrete-event simulator to explore the future consequences of scheduling decisions within a strict time budget, adapting to the configured reward at each scheduling cycle. We evaluate MARS on year-long production workloads from two systems at Argonne Leadership Computing Facility -- 4,360-node Theta and 560-node Polaris---under two reward functions: wait-time minimization (MARS-CW) and utilization maximization (MARS-CU). Unlike DRL and heuristics, which only react to the current queue or wait for backfill to find holes, MARS exploits look-ahead to proactively drain the system and plan around future reservations, packing the system to avoid the fragmentation and utilization drop that typically precede reservation windows. MARS-CW reduces tail wait time by 64% on Theta and 43% on Polaris over the production WFP heuristic, while MARS-CU recovers utilization in the 48 hours leading into maintenance, demonstrating that MARS can target either objective via reward reconfiguration.

</details>


### [366] [DGEMM with Ozaki Scheme I/II on FP4 Tensor Cores: A Base-13 E2M1 Limb Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06812)
*Shun-ichiro Hayashi,Daichi Mukunoki,Tetsuya Hoshino,Takahiro Katagiri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出在FP4（E2M1）Tensor Core上构建Ozaki方案I和II来模拟FP64矩阵乘法（DGEMM），利用FP4值加倍后为整数且按13的倍数平移可覆盖所有整数这一关键性质，实现FP4 Tensor Core上的高精度矩阵乘法，并在RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell上验证了性能与FP8实现竞争甚至在大规模问题上超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往基于INT8和FP8的低精度Tensor Core模拟FP64矩阵乘法的实现已经存在，但更快、更低的FP4精度尚未被利用。由于FP4 Tensor Core吞吐量理论上可达FP8的两倍，如何将FP4用于高精度矩阵乘法模拟是缺失的关键环节。

Method: 利用FP4（E2M1）值加倍后成为整数以及将该整数集合按13的倍数平移可覆盖所有整数的性质，将任意整数转换为以13为基的FP4 limb序列，使得在FP32累加器中中间和保持无误差，从而在FP4 Tensor Core上实现Ozaki方案I和II。同时利用相同原理在FP4 Tensor Core上实现INT8 Tensor Core整数GEMM的位精确模拟。此外，提出了提高峰值性能达成率的kernel实现优化。

Result: 在RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell上验证，FP4上的Ozaki方案II性能与现有FP8实现相当，在16384³的大规模问题上实际性能超过FP8实现；FP4 Tensor Core吞吐量为FP8两倍时，理论上FP4的Ozaki方案II略优于FP8对应实现，加上kernel优化后进一步获得实测加速。

Conclusion: FP4 Tensor Core能够有效用于Ozaki方案I和II实现FP64矩阵乘法模拟，且通过基13整数分解性质和针对性的kernel优化，在吞吐量优势下可获得优于FP8实现的性能，为低精度Tensor Core上的高精度计算开辟了新途径。

Abstract: This paper proposes a method and its implementation for emulating FP64 matrix multiplication (DGEMM) by constructing, on FP4 (E2M1; 2 exponent bits and 1 mantissa bit) Tensor Cores, Ozaki schemes I and II, which realize high-precision matrix multiplication on low-precision arithmetic units. Prior implementations were based on INT8 and FP8, and the use of the faster FP4 had not been realized. The key property is that every FP4 value becomes an integer when doubled, and that shifting this integer set by multiples of 13 covers all integers. Converting an arbitrary integer into base-13 FP4 limbs by this property keeps intermediate sums error-free in FP32 accumulators, which makes FP4 Tensor Cores usable for Ozaki schemes I and II. By the same principle, the integer GEMM of INT8 Tensor Cores can also be emulated bit-exactly on FP4 Tensor Cores. When FP4 Tensor Cores have twice the throughput of FP8, Ozaki scheme II on FP4 theoretically achieves slightly higher performance than its FP8 counterpart. This paper further proposes kernel implementation optimizations raising the attained fraction of peak performance, obtaining a measured speedup on top of the theoretical advantage. We verify this on an RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell, achieving performance competitive with that of an existing FP8-based implementation of Ozaki scheme II, and actually exceeding it at a large problem size ($16384^3$).

</details>


### [367] [StateFlow: Sequence Pipeline Parallelism for Long-Context Modeling with Linear Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06838)
*Wenxuan Zhao,Yingfa Chen,Xu Han,Wenjing Han,Tianbo Huang,Zhiyu Li,Ao Sun,Jingheng Xu,Lin Gan,Guangwen Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: StateFlow是一种针对具有线性循环结构的模型（如线性注意力、状态空间模型及混合模型）设计的序列流水线并行训练系统。它通过将序列切分为块、传播边界状态和梯度、使用profile引导的非均匀分块以及重叠状态转换来计算，实现了高达2.22倍的吞吐量提升和2.45倍的内存减少，支持多达32B参数和256K上下文长度的训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文训练对大型语言模型日益重要，线性注意力和状态空间模型被广泛用于提升长上下文效率。然而，对于循环模型和混合模型的长序列训练，高效并行化仍然是一个挑战，传统的并行流水线方法在激活生命周期和计算负载均衡方面存在瓶颈。

Method: StateFlow将每个序列划分为多个块（chunks），调度这些块的执行同时跨块传播边界状态和梯度，从而缩短激活生命周期并提升训练吞吐量。此外，StateFlow采用profile引导的非均匀分块策略来平衡混合模型中循环注意力和softmax注意力计算之间的负载，并将并行度有限的边界状态转换与周围计算进行重叠，以充分利用计算资源。

Result: 将StateFlow应用于高达32B参数和256K上下文长度的模型，相比传统流水线并行方法，实现了最高2.22倍的吞吐量提升和2.45倍的内存减少，使得原本不可行的配置变得可训练。

Conclusion: StateFlow通过序列流水线并行、非均匀分块和计算重叠等优化策略，有效地解决了循环和混合模型的长序列训练并行化难题，显著提升了训练效率和内存利用率，为超长上下文模型的训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Long-context training is increasingly important for large language models, and linear attention and state space models have become popular for improving long-context efficiency. However, efficiently parallelizing long-sequence training for recurrent and hybrid models remains challenging.
  We present StateFlow, a sequence pipeline parallelism system for models with linear recurrence. StateFlow partitions each sequence into chunks and schedules their execution while propagating boundary states and gradients across chunks, thereby reducing activation lifetimes and improving training throughput. StateFlow further uses profile-guided nonuniform chunking to balance recurrence and softmax attention computation in hybrid models, and overlaps state transitions that expose limited parallelism with surrounding computation. Applying StateFlow to models with up to 32B parameters and 256K context length, we achieve up to \(2.22\times\) throughput improvements and \(2.45\times\) memory reduction compared to conventional pipeline parallelism, enabling otherwise infeasible configurations.

</details>


### [368] [Stream Learning: Partition-Fair Gossip Learning Without Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06946)
*Fabien Mathieu,Alexandre Pham,Maria Gradinariu Potop-Butucaru,S{é}bastien Tixeuil*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Stream Learning协议族，其中最简策略（Ri：向随机邻居发送本地训练最少的模型分区）在无故障场景下与现有最优的PTGL协议持平，在30%节点永久崩溃及数据异构场景下性能超越PTGL最多5.53%（HAR）和5.41%（MNIST），且无需token计数器或元数据交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTGL协议依赖token-based公平机制与per-neighbor元数据交换来调度模型分区传播，机制复杂且开销大。作者通过类比P2P直播流媒体中视频块稀缺性调度，探索更简洁高效的分区调度策略，以降低协议复杂度同时提升鲁棒性。

Method: 将gossip学习中的模型分区类比为直播视频块、分区年龄类比为块稀缺性，由此构建"两阶段选择策略"设计空间（先选分区或先选邻居），实例化出十种具体协议统称Stream Learning。核心策略Ri为：选择本地训练程度最低的分区发送给均匀随机邻居，放弃了token计数器和邻居元数据交换。

Result: 最简策略Ri在无故障工作负载下与PTGL性能持平；在最优30%节点永久崩溃的对抗场景中，Ri在所有完全图配置下匹配或超越PTGL，在Dirichlet β=0.1的最高异构度下差距达HAR 5.53%、MNIST 5.41%。实验表明分区公平性（由单一局部年龄规则实现）是差距来源；token-based速率控制与效用最大化不仅未带来增益，在异构场景下反而低于该简单规则。

Conclusion: 分区年龄这一简单局部规则足以实现分区公平性，复杂机制（token计数、元数据交换、效用最大化）在gossip学习分区调度中并非必要甚至有害。Stream Learning中基于"最少训练分区优先"的最简策略为分布式机器学习的鲁棒性提供了更简洁且更优的设计方案。

Abstract: In gossip learning, a network of nodes trains a shared model collaboratively, without a central coordinator, by repeatedly exchanging parts of their local models. The state-of-the-art protocol, Partitioned Token Gossip Learning (PTGL) of Heged{ü}s et al., splits the weight matrix into S fixed partitions and disseminates them using a token-based fairness mechanism coupled with per-neighbor metadata exchange. We revisit partition scheduling by analogy with peer-to-peer live streaming, where model partitions act as video chunks and partition age acts as chunk scarcity. The analogy yields a design space of two-stage selection strategies (partition first, or neighbor first), from which we instantiate ten concrete protocols collectively called Stream Learning. Our main finding is that the simplest of these protocols, which transmits the locally least-trained partition to a uniformly random neighbor (Ri), matches PTGL on fault-free workloads while requiring neither token counters nor metadata exchange. Under an adversarial 30% permanent crash of the best-performing nodes, Ri matches or outperforms PTGL across all complete-graph configurations tested, with the gap reaching 5.53% on HAR and 5.41% on MNIST in the most heterogeneous regime (Dirichlet $β$ = 0.1). In our experiments, partition fairness, captured by a single local rule on partition age, accounts for the gap; token-based rate control and utility maximization do not improve over this rule and, under heterogeneity, sit below it.

</details>


### [369] [A Self-Adaptive Extensible CEP framework for the Cloud-Edge Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06966)
*Olaf Markus Link,Sukanya Bhowmik*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展的自适应CEP框架，具备负载调节功能，帮助算子在异构和资源受限环境中应对动态变化的工作负载和过载情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CEP系统部署在云边连续体上的运营商图面临动态变化的工作负载，异构节点间负载不均衡可能导致个别算子处理过载而无法适应增长的工作量，亟需一个能实时检测并适应工作负载变化的框架。

Method: 提出一个可扩展的自适应CEP框架，集成负载调节机制，使算子在过载情况下仍能持续工作，从而支持在异构和/或资源受限环境中的算子部署。

Result: 该框架使分布于云边连续体的算子即使在过载情况下也能维持运行，适应动态变化的负载分布。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应CEP框架通过负载调节特性有效应对异构/资源受限环境下的算子过载问题，保障CEP系统在云边连续体上的鲁棒性和可持续运行。

Abstract: Traditional complex event processing (CEP) systems focus on extracting information out of simple events in the input event streams to detect complex event patterns. In the context of CEP, a computing unit that executes a complex data transformation task is called an operator. Typically, a CEP system consists of an operator graph that could be distributed over the cloud-edge continuum. However, an operator graph distributed over heterogeneous nodes comes with its own set of challenges. In fact, the nodes of an operator graph are typically subjected to dynamically changing workloads. Unbalanced load distributions across heterogeneous nodes may lead to processing overloads in individual operators that do not have enough resources to properly adapt to an increasing workload. As a result, a framework that can detect and adapt to dynamically changing workload in real time across the various nodes of the cloud-edge continuum becomes paramount. To this end, the goal of this work is to propose a new extensible self-adaptive CEP framework with load regulatory features that help operators continue working even in face of an overload, thus facilitating the deployment of operators in a heterogeneous and/or resource-limited environment.

</details>


### [370] [A Kubernetes Scheduler Plugin for Cluster-Wide Placement Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06987)
*Henrik Daniel Christensen,Saverio Giallorenzo,Jacopo Mauro*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: OPSche是一个开源的Kubernetes Scheduling Framework插件，允许外部求解器与默认调度器协同进行集群级放置决策，通过协调的框架钩子原子性地验证和执行求解器生成的放置方案，支持三种触发模式（调度失败、周期、稳定队列），可与基于约束的优化求解器配合，将资源使用率提升最高3.0%，调度延迟降低超过一秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes默认调度器采用快速、局部的放置决策，导致资源碎片化、集群利用率降低和过度配置。外部求解器虽能计算全局放置方案，但在上游集群中执行这些方案很困难：Kubernetes没有原生的跨节点抢占机制、无协调的并发调度会导致不一致，且替换默认调度器会使部署与上游发布周期脱节。

Method: OPSche作为Kubernetes Scheduling Framework插件实现，允许外部求解器与默认调度器协同驱动集群级放置决策。它通过协调的框架钩子原子性地验证和执行求解器产生的方案，支持三种触发器模式：调度失败触发、固定周期触发、待处理工作负载稳定时触发，每种模式还有阻塞变体以精细调节放置质量、延迟和中断。

Result: 与基于约束的优化求解器配对，OPSche在广泛的集群配置中验证了可行性，资源使用率提升最高达3.0%，调度延迟减少超过一秒。

Conclusion: OPSche解决了Kubernetes集群中外部全局求解器与默认调度器之间的集成难题，在不替换默认调度器的情况下实现集群级优化放置，为资源碎片化和利用率问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The default scheduler of Kubernetes, the state-of-the-art container orchestrator, uses fast, local placement decisions. Unfortunately, this design leads to resource fragmentation, reduced cluster usage, and overprovisioning. External solvers can compute global placement plans, but enforcing these plans in upstream clusters is hard. Kubernetes provides no native cross-node preemption, uncoordinated concurrent scheduling leads to inconsistencies, and replacing the default scheduler would sever deployments from upstream cycles. We present OPSche, an open-source Kubernetes Scheduling Framework plugin where external solvers can drive cluster-wide placement decisions in concert with the default scheduler. OPSche atomically validates and enforces solver-produced plans through coordinated framework hooks and supports three trigger modes: scheduling-failure, periodic, and stable-queue -- resp. triggered when a workload cannot be placed, at fixed time intervals, when the set of pending workloads stabilises. Each mode has a blocking variant for a finer tuning of placement quality, latency, and disruption. We pair OPSche with a constraint-based optimisation solver, showing its feasibility across a broad set of cluster configurations and reporting improvements of resource usage by up to 3.0% and scheduling latency by more than a second.

</details>


### [371] [HiSparse: Scaling Sparse-Attention Decoding with Hierarchical KV Cache Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07009)
*Zhiqiang Xie,Zhangheng Huang,Tingwei Huang,Ziyi Xu,Ruiyang Ma,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HiSparse是一个精确的、与索引器无关的分层KV缓存系统，用于稀疏注意力服务。它将每个请求的完整KV历史存储在主机内存中，用少量固定大小的GPU缓存限定解码内存占用，通过融合CUDA内核在解码CUDA图中解析每层的选择（命中检测、LRU替换、主机到设备传输），并利用跨层共享选择的模型做精确的逐层预取来隐藏约一半的剩余未命中开销。系统已合入SGLang上游代码库，在H200、B200和GH200平台上对三种稀疏注意力族（DSA、NSA、Quest）进行了评估，长上下文工作负载的峰值生成吞吐量最高提升4.7倍，同时保持可比的每token延迟并降低高负载下的首token时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稀疏注意力服务系统虽然计算上很廉价，但KV缓存仍然全部驻留在GPU HBM中，导致内存随上下文长度线性增长，在计算资源耗尽之前就遇到容量墙，甚至超出HBM的上下文根本无法服务。

Method: HiSparse采用分层KV缓存设计：完整KV历史放在主机内存（host memory），GPU上仅保留小型固定大小缓存（bounded residency）；通过融合CUDA内核在解码CUDA图中完成每层的选择解析（hit检测、LRU替换、H2D传输）；对跨层共享选择的模型，利用精确的逐层预取（layer-wise prefetching）隐藏约一半的剩余未命中延迟。由于只改变KV的存放位置而不改变计算逻辑，模型输出与原始实现完全一致。

Result: 在H200、B200、GH200平台上对DSA、NSA和Quest三种稀疏注意力族进行评估，长上下文工作负载的峰值生成吞吐量最高提升4.7倍，每token延迟保持可比，高负载下TTFT降低。无IO的oracle实验表明解析机制本身不增加可测量的每token开销，主机-设备IO是受限驻留的唯一代价。

Conclusion: HiSparse通过将KV缓存从GPU HBM转移到主机内存并采用固定大小的GPU缓存，打破了稀疏注意力服务的内存容量墙，实现了对完整上下文的精确可服务性，同时保持输出一致性（exactness），是上游SGLang已合入的实用解决方案。

Abstract: Top-k sparse attention makes long-context LLM decoding cheap to compute: each step reads only a few thousand selected KV entries rather than the full context. Serving systems, however, typically keep the entire KV cache in GPU HBM so that every position stays selectable, so a request's memory bill still grows with its full context length--decoding hits a capacity wall long before it runs out of compute, and a context whose KV cache exceeds HBM cannot be served at all. We present HiSparse, an exact, indexer-agnostic hierarchical KV cache for sparse-attention serving. HiSparse keeps each request's full KV history in host memory and bounds its decode footprint with a small, fixed-size GPU cache; a fused CUDA kernel resolves each layer's selections--hit detection, LRU replacement, and host-to-device fetches--inside the decode CUDA graph; and, for models that share selections across layers, exact layer-wise prefetching hides roughly half of the remaining miss overhead. Because only KV placement changes, model outputs are unchanged. HiSparse is merged into upstream SGLang and evaluated across three sparse-attention families (DSA, NSA, and Quest) on H200, B200, and GH200 platforms: it improves peak generation throughput by up to 4.7x on long-context workloads while preserving comparable per-token latency and reducing time-to-first-token at high load--and a no-IO oracle shows the resolution mechanism itself adds no measurable per-token cost, leaving host-device IO as the only price of bounded residency.

</details>


### [372] [Scalable High-Fidelity Macromolecular Docking for GPU-Accelerated Supercomputers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07078)
*Xiangyu Meng,Peng Chen,Mingzhen Li,Jianmin Wang,Sen Wang,Guangming Tan,Weile Jia,Mohamed Wahib,Tao Luo,Xun Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SparkleDock是一个基于GSO的可扩展柔性分子对接框架，通过重设计GSO以暴露细粒度并行性、将能量打分计算重构为Tensor Core兼容矩阵运算，并引入性能模型驱动的调度与负载均衡，在A100/H100单GPU上分别实现9.7×和18.9×加速，在512 GPU上实现超过两个数量级的加速，将对接时间从数小时缩短至秒级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性分子对接虽然能提供高保真的生物分子相互作用预测，但在大规模场景下计算成本过高。现有方法LightDock虽然利用GSO算法获得了较高的准确性，但其有限的并行性、不规则的计算模式以及严重的负载不均衡问题，使其无法在GPU超级计算机上高效执行，亟需一种可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 1) 重新设计GSO算法，在glowworm智能体层面暴露大规模细粒度并行性；2) 将占主导地位的能量打分计算重构为Tensor Core兼容的矩阵运算形式，通过结构化矩阵运算高效处理不规则配对交互；3) 引入性能模型驱动的调度策略，实现负载均衡及跨GPU的out-of-core扩展。

Result: 在单张A100和H100 GPU上分别比LightDock快9.7倍和18.9倍；在大规模并行下实现超过两个数量级的加速；在512块GPU上，将对接时间从数小时降至秒级。

Conclusion: SparkleDock使得大规模、高保真的柔性对接虚拟筛选成为可能，解决了此前柔性对接在超大规模GPU计算上无法实际应用的核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Flexible macromolecular docking offers high-fidelity predictions of biomolecular interactions, but remains prohibitively expensive at scale. Among existing approaches, LightDock leverages Glowworm Swarm Optimization (GSO) for accuracy, yet suffers from limited parallelism, irregular computation, and severe load imbalance, preventing efficient execution on GPU supercomputers. We present SparkleDock, a scalable GSO-based docking framework enabling near-real-time flexible docking. We redesign GSO to expose massive fine-grained parallelism at the glowworm-agent level, and restructure the dominant energy scoring computation into a Tensor Core-compatible formulation, enabling efficient execution of irregular pairwise interactions through structured matrix operations. We further introduce a performance-model-driven scheduling for load balancing and out-of-core scaling across GPUs. SparkleDock achieves 9.7 $\times$ and 18.9 $\times$ speedups over LightDock on single A100 and H100 GPU, and delivers over two orders of magnitude acceleration at scale. On 512 GPUs, it reduces docking time from hours to seconds, enabling large-scale, high-fidelity virtual screening previously impractical with flexible docking.

</details>


### [373] [Fast end-to-end cloud application cold-start with initscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07358)
*Ariel Szekely,Robert Morris,M. Frans Kaashoek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 initscripts，一种让开发者以脚本方式指定应用初始化例程的接口，云平台可通过重叠 setup 和 initialization 两个阶段来降低 serverless 函数的冷启动延迟，平均加速可达 1.67X-1.81X。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Serverless 函数频繁遭遇冷启动，且启动延迟可分解为平台层的 setup 和应用层的 initialization 两个组成部分。由于许多函数是短时运行的，启动延迟往往主导其执行延迟，因此有必要对这两部分进行优化。

Method: 提出 initscripts 机制：开发者通过脚本接口将应用的初始化例程告知云平台，平台可以重叠执行 setup 和 initialization 步骤；应用运行后，initscript 将初始化结果传递给应用以完成引导。

Result: 在 ServerlessBench Python 应用中，冷启动平均加速 1.67X（无需修改应用）；serverless 图像识别工作负载加速 1.81X；off-the-shelf 微服务（如 etcd 和 memcached）的启动时间也有显著提升。

Conclusion: initscripts 通过解耦和重叠平台的 setup 阶段与应用自身的 initialization 阶段，能有效降低 serverless 冷启动延迟，且对多种应用类型（Python 函数、图像识别、微服务）均有显著加速效果，无需修改应用本身。

Abstract: Serverless functions are a popular way of deploying cloud applications. Because many of these functions are short- running and experience frequent cold-starts, start latencies often dominate their execution latency. Start latency can be broken down into two components: setup and initialization. Setup involves steps the cloud platform takes when starting an application, such as downloading its binary and creating an isolated execution environment. Initialization involves steps the application takes after it has started running but before it can do useful work, such as connecting to other services, coordinating to claim work, and downloading inputs.
  This paper contributes initscripts, which provide a script- able interface for developers to specify their application's initialization routine to the cloud platform. The platform can then run an application's initscript and reduce application start latency by overlapping setup and initialization steps. Once the application is up and running, the initscript bootstraps the application by transferring initialization results to it. Using initscripts, we were able to speed up cold-starts of several of the ServerlessBench Python applications by an average 1.67X with no modifications to the application. Initscripts also speed up start times for a serverless image recognition workload by 1.81X, and off-the-shelf microservices like etcd and memcached.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [374] [A Low-Latency ASIC Architecture for Real-Time Line Segment Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06439)
*Amir Hossein Jalilvand,Parsa Hassani Shariat Panahi,M. Hassan Najafi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于步长（step-length）算法的低延迟ASIC架构，用于实时线段检测。该架构采用五项ASIC专用设计特征，实现全流水线化、每时钟周期处理一个像素的确定性延迟。在45nm CMOS工艺下综合，VGA分辨率下达到325 FPS（125MHz时为406 FPS），Full HD下为48 FPS，功耗25.54mW，面积0.412mm²，较90nm Hough变换ASIC实现功耗降低49%、帧率提升1.6倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习方法在嵌入式视觉应用中虽精度高但资源消耗大，难以部署在资源受限平台上；经典算法虽高效但存在内容相关的延迟波动。现有方案无法同时满足边缘计算场景对实时性、低功耗和小面积的需求，因此需要一种专用硬件架构来实现高能效的实时线段检测。

Method: 基于步长（step-length）算法设计全流水线ASIC架构，集成五项ASIC专用优化特征：(1) 基于寄存器的行缓冲数据复用；(2) 无乘法器的MCM（Multiple Constant Multiplication）滤波；(3) 8类角度量化；(4) 类CAM（Content-Addressable Memory）关联存储器实现单周期匹配；(5) 优化的重复移除机制。架构每时钟周期处理一个像素，具有确定性延迟。

Result: 在45nm CMOS工艺下综合实现：VGA分辨率下325 FPS、Full HD下48 FPS，功耗25.54mW，面积0.412mm²；在125MHz时钟下VGA分辨率帧率提升至406 FPS，功耗31.48mW。与90nm技术节点基于Line Hough Transform的ASIC实现相比，功耗降低49%，帧率提升1.6倍以上。

Conclusion: 该架构在功耗、面积和实时性能之间取得了良好平衡，验证了基于经典算法进行硬件专用化设计的有效性，适用于自主导航、视觉SLAM和工业检测等边缘计算场景中的实时线段检测需求。

Abstract: Line segment detection is a critical preprocessing step in embedded vision applications such as autonomous navigation, visual SLAM, and industrial inspection. Deep learning methods achieve high accuracy but require substantial resources, limiting their deployment on resource-constrained platforms. Classical algorithms are efficient but exhibit content-dependent latency. This paper presents a low-latency ASIC architecture for real-time line segment detection. The proposed design is based on the step-length algorithm and incorporates five ASIC-specific features: register-based line buffering with data reuse, multiplierless MCM-based filtering, 8-class angle quantization, a CAM-like associative memory for single-cycle matching, and an optimized duplicate removal mechanism. The architecture is fully pipelined and processes one pixel per clock cycle with deterministic latency. Synthesized in a 45nm CMOS process, the design achieves 325 FPS at VGA resolution and 48 FPS at Full HD, with 25.54 mW power consumption and 0.412 mm\textsuperscript{2} area. At 125 MHz, the throughput increases to 406 FPS at VGA resolution with 31.48 mW power consumption. Compared with a 90nm ASIC implementation based on the Line Hough Transform, the proposed design reduces power consumption by 49\% and delivers over 1.6 times higher frame rate. The architecture is well suited for edge-computing applications requiring real-time performance, low power, and minimal area.

</details>


### [375] [Retention-Aware RISC-V ISA Extension and Memory Controller on FPGA for MLC NVM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06725)
*Mina Ibrahim,Martel Shokry,Lokesh Siddhu,Lars Bauer,Hassan Nassar,Joerg Henkel*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向MLC NVM的内存控制器架构和RISC-V指令集扩展，通过平衡写入速度与保留时间来优化NVM写操作。设计了基于状态机的AXI接口控制器、快速存储指令以及按位重要性感知的AXI从设备外设，在FPGA上实现并验证，硬件开销降低30%，流式工作负载性能提升超过7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLC NVM提供了写入延迟与保留时间之间的权衡（快速写入保留时间短，慢速写入保留时间长），但现有工作缺乏在系统层面基于硬件验证和原型实现来利用这种权衡的研究。

Method: 1) 设计基于有限状态机（FSM）的定制NVM控制器，采用AXI内存映射接口，通过增强的突发传输优化读写操作；2) 在RISC-V中引入快速存储（fast-store）指令以提升写性能并解决保留时间限制；3) 设计按位重要性感知的AXI从设备外设：关键位（如MSB）使用慢速高保留写入，非关键位（如LSB）使用快速低保留写入，在增强性能的同时保证数据可靠性；4) 在FPGA平台上进行硬件实现与验证。

Result: 实验结果表明：控制器相比传统设计硬件开销降低30%；快速存储指令在流式工作负载下性能提升超过7%，且硬件开销小于0.08%；按位AXI外设在64x64矩阵时LUT利用率低于3.5%，32x32时低于1%，具备集成到更大SoC的可行性。

Conclusion: 通过硬件实现验证了系统级利用MLC NVM写入速度与保留时间权衡的可行性，所提出的控制器架构、RISC-V指令扩展和按位重要性感知外设在性能提升、硬件开销和数据可靠性之间取得了良好的平衡，适合大规模系统集成。

Abstract: Non-volatile memory (NVM) technologies, particularly Multi-Level Cell (MLC) NVMs, offer significant potential for increasing memory density. MLC NVMs provide a tradeoff between write latency and retention time, where faster writes/stores result in lower retention and slower writes yield higher retention. However, limited work has been done to validate and prototype NVM-based systems in hardware, leveraging this tradeoff at the system level. In this paper, we present a novel memory controller architecture and a RISC-V instruction set extension to optimize MLC NVM write operations by balancing speed and retention time. Our custom NVM controller, built around a finite state machine with an AXI memory-mapped interface, efficiently manages read/write operations with enhanced burst transfers, minimizing latency. Additionally, we introduce a fast-store instruction in RISC-V to increasing write performance while addressing retention limitations. Further, we design a dedicated AXI slave peripheral that supports bit-significance-aware writes: critical bits (e.g., MSBs) are written using slower, high-retention writes, while non-critical bits (e.g., LSBs) use faster, low-retention writes to help enhance performance without compromising data reliability. These enhancements are implemented in hardware on an FPGA platform. Experimental results show that our controller reduces hardware overhead by 30% compared to conventional designs, and the fast-store instruction improves performance by over 7% for streaming workloads with less than 0.08% hardware overhead. The bit-wise AXI peripheral has a LUT utilization staying below 3.5% even for 64x64 matrices, and under 1% for 32x32 sizes, making it viable for integration into larger SoCs.

</details>


### [376] [HLSmith: An Expert-Guided Agentic Framework for C/C++-to-HLS Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06791)
*Yuebo Luo,Ahmad Sedigh Baroughi,Philip Stachura,Le Chen,Venkatram Vishwanath,Zhenman Fang,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出HLSmith框架，通过HLS优化专家知识库、分阶段反馈驱动编排流程和工具接地模型适配流水线，将C/C++程序自动翻译为优化的HLS加速器，在PolyBench上相比ChatHLS取得4.24倍几何平均加速，且100%设计功能正确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应用特定的FPGA加速器虽能带来显著的性能和能效提升，但开发成本高昂且需要大量硬件专业知识。即使使用高层次综合（HLS），设计者仍需丰富的硬件经验才能构建高性能加速器。虽然大语言模型（LLM）在软件生成方面表现突出，但即便是前沿模型也缺乏将基准C/C++程序可靠转换为高性能HLS设计所需的硬件直觉和程序性知识，难以识别有效架构、遵循HLS专家的优化流程并在不同内核上一致地应用硬件变换。

Method: HLSmith包含三个核心组件：(1) HLS优化专家知识库，编码有守卫的变换配方、适用性和前置条件以及应避免的不安全情况；(2) 分阶段、反馈驱动的编排流程，模拟专家HLS开发实践，引导代理完成综合、瓶颈分析和优化；(3) 工具接地的模型适配流水线，将来自商业前沿模型的优化轨迹转换为微调开放权重LLM的训练数据。

Result: 在PolyBench基准上，HLSmith相比ChatHLS取得4.24倍几何平均加速，并在软件和RTL仿真中对每个基准都产生功能正确的设计，而ChatHLS的合法设计率仅为57%。使用商业前沿模型和开放权重模型时，HLSmith分别实现高达252倍和138倍的加速。

Conclusion: HLSmith通过将专家知识系统化编码并集成到LLM驱动的编排框架中，有效弥合了LLM与HLS专家之间的差距，显著提升了自动化HLS加速器生成的质量、可靠性和性能，为LLM辅助硬件设计提供了可扩展的范式。

Abstract: Application-specific FPGA accelerators offer substantial performance and energy-efficiency gains across many application domains, but developing them is costly, often requiring months of specialized effort. Even with high-level synthesis (HLS), designers still need extensive hardware expertise to build high-performance accelerators. Although large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong software-generation capabilities, even frontier models lack the hardware intuition and procedural knowledge needed to reliably translate baseline C/C++ programs into high-performance HLS designs: they struggle to identify effective architectures, follow the optimization processes used by HLS experts, and apply hardware transformations consistently across diverse kernels. We present HLSmith, an expert-guided framework for translating C/C++ programs into optimized HLS accelerators. HLSmith combines three components: an HLS optimization expertise library that encodes guarded transformation recipes, their applicability and prerequisite conditions, and unsafe cases to avoid; a staged, feedback-driven orchestration flow modeled on expert HLS development practice that guides agents through synthesis, bottleneck analysis, and optimization; and a tool-grounded model-adaptation pipeline that converts optimization trajectories from commercial frontier models into training data for fine-tuning open-weight LLMs. We evaluate HLSmith on PolyBench against ChatHLS, a leading prior agent-orchestration framework for HLS accelerator development. HLSmith achieves a geometric mean speedup of 4.24x over ChatHLS while producing functionally correct designs, in both software and RTL simulation, for every benchmark, compared with ChatHLS's 57% valid-design rate. It further reaches speedups of up to 252x and 138x with commercial frontier models and open-weight models, respectively.

</details>


### [377] [G-Power: Architecture-level GPU Power Modeling with Aggregated Knowledge Foundations from Known GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06870)
*Qijun Zhang,Yao Lu,Shang Liu,Mengming Li,Chen Zhang,Dongbo Wang,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: G-Power是一个架构级GPU功耗建模框架，通过利用额外已知GPU芯片的知识迁移（预训练→注意力聚合→微调三阶段），解决了传统模型因仅用微基准测试训练导致精度低的问题，在四款现代NVIDIA GPU上平均MAPE为14%、相关系数R为0.88，显著优于AccelWattch。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU功耗优化需要快速的架构级功耗评估模型，但功耗标签收集耗时导致只能用简单微基准测试训练模型，而这种训练数据的局限性使现有架构级GPU功耗模型（如AccelWattch）精度较低。

Method: G-Power采用三阶段算法：1) 在额外已知GPU芯片上进行预训练以构建知识基础；2) 使用注意力启发的聚合机制捕获目标GPU与已知芯片之间的相似性；3) 在目标GPU上进行微调以适应特定芯片的功耗特征。

Result: 在四款现代NVIDIA GPU上评估，G-Power平均MAPE低至14%，相关系数R高达0.88，相比AccelWattch分别降低了22%的MAPE并提升了0.36的R值。

Conclusion: 通过跨芯片知识迁移与注意力聚合机制，G-Power能有效克服微基准训练数据的局限性，实现更精确的架构级GPU功耗建模。

Abstract: Graphics Processing Units (GPUs) have been serving as critical computation resources for large-scale parallel computations. With increasing chip complexity, power efficiency has become an important design objective for modern GPUs. GPU power optimization relies on fast power evaluation, requiring architecture-level GPU power model. However, because of the time-consuming power label collection, only simple microbenchmarks are adopted for training. The limitation of microbenchmarks as training data incurs low accuracy for existing architecture-level GPU power models like AccelWattch.
  To address the limitation of microbenchmarks as training data, we propose G-Power, an architecture-level GPU power modeling framework that utilizes additional known GPU chips to provide additional knowledge. G-Power utilizes the aggregated knowledge foundation from additional known GPU chips and then performs fine-tuning on our target GPU. To provide foundations with additional known GPU chips and capture the similarity to utilize these foundations for fine-tuning, G-Power adopts a three-phase algorithm consisting of 1) pre-training with additional known chips, 2) attention-inspired aggregation, and 3) fine-tuning on our target GPU. We evaluate G-Power on four modern NVIDIA GPUs, demonstrating high accuracy. G-Power can achieve a low MAPE of 14% and a high correlation coefficient R of 0.88 on average, which are 22% lower MAPE and 0.36 higher R than AccelWattch.

</details>


### [378] [Rethinking Unified Memory for NPU-PIM Systems: Dual-View Memory for Dynamic Inference of LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06989)
*Shixin Zhao,Lian Liu,Tianhua Han,Mengdi Wang,Yinhe Han,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: PFM提出了一种面向NPU-PIM异构架构的双视图内存系统，通过解耦物理数据布局与逻辑视图，支持同一张量在不同执行设备间动态切换，无需数据复制或重布局，从而提升LLM推理吞吐量最高达2.32倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NPU-PIM统一内存设计假设每个张量绑定固定的执行设备，采用静态的设备偏向数据映射。然而在LLM推理中，由于prefill/decode阶段切换和MoE路由等动态行为，同一张量的最优执行设备会在运行时变化，导致设备偏向映射与真实访问模式不匹配，造成带宽利用不足和性能损失。

Method: PFM采用双视图内存系统：1）将数据存储在联合优化的物理布局中；2）对NPU和PIM分别暴露不同的逻辑视图，实现跨设备高效访问而无需数据复制或重布局；3）设计了访问者感知的地址转换机制和运行时调度机制，以支持LLM工作负载波动时最优执行设备动态变化的场景。

Result: 在多种LLM上的评估显示，PFM相比现有方案将端到端吞吐量提升了最高2.32倍，证明了其在NPU-PIM统一内存管理方面的有效性和广泛适用性。

Conclusion: PFM通过解耦物理布局与逻辑视图，打破了传统统一内存设计中张量-设备静态绑定的限制，为NPU-PIM异构系统的动态执行提供了高效的内存管理方案。

Abstract: Heterogeneous architectures that combine neural processing unit (NPU) and processing-in-memory (PIM) are increasingly adopted to accelerate LLM inference. Prior work focuses on building a unified memory that allows NPUs and PIM to share data without duplication. However, these designs implicitly assume that each tensor is bound to a fixed execution device, and therefore rely on static, device-biased data mappings.
  We observe that this assumption does not hold in modern LLM workloads. Due to phase changes (e.g., prefill vs. decode) and dynamic behaviors such as MoE routing, the optimal execution device for the same tensor can change at runtime. Under such dynamic execution, device-biased mappings become mismatched to access patterns, leading to substantial bandwidth underutilization and performance loss. This paper presents PFM (PIM-as-Flexible-Memory), a dual-view memory system that decouples physical data layout from accessor-visible logical views. PFM stores data in a jointly optimized physical layout and exposes different logical interpretations to NPUs and PIM, enabling efficient access across devices without data duplication or relayout. We further design accessor-aware address translation and runtime scheduling mechanisms to support dynamic execution when LLM workloads fluctuate and the optimal execution device dynamically changes. Our evaluation across LLMs shows that PFM improves end-to-end throughput by up to 2.32$\times$, demonstrating its effectiveness and broad applicability as a unified memory management solution for NPU-PIM systems.

</details>


### [379] [Dual-Node NVIDIA DGX Spark over Tailscale: A Remote-Access Testbed for Distributed LLM Training and Cyber-Threat-Intelligence Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07226)
*Vasanth Iyer*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个在两台NVIDIA DGX Spark系统上通过200 Gb/s直接光纤链路实现分布式NanoChat预训练的概念验证部署，并展示了该集群同时支持网络安全微调数据集构建、课程教学等多样用途的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型AI系统让本地语言模型实验日益普及，但桌面级加速器上进行多节点训练的实践证据仍然有限。作者希望通过实际部署填补这一空白，为小型实验室提供可复现的分布式训练参考，并验证本地基础设施能否同时支撑研究与教学需求。

Method: 使用两台NVIDIA DGX Spark系统（各配备GB10 Grace Blackwell SoC和128GB统一内存），通过Tailscale mesh VPN远程管理和200 Gb/s QSFP56直接光纤链路连接。配置PyTorch torchrun、DDP和NCCL，每节点一个进程，深度20的NanoChat模型，每节点本地批大小32，2048 token上下文。同时从77份CISA公告构建网络安全微调数据集，使用Ollama托管的LLM评判器进行17问评估。

Result: 训练步时间约69.4秒（约1890 tokens/s），四天处理约6.53亿tokens。CTI增强检查点在特定类别上有所提升但通用知识类别出现回退，整体评分从2.06小幅升至2.29（0-10分制）。集群同时服务于CS 426课程和CompTIA Security+ POGIL查询引擎。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在桌面级硬件上实现多节点分布式训练和多样化用途的可行性，但作者明确表示这是可行性验证而非扩展效率声明，因为单节点吞吐量是估算值而非匹配条件下实测值。提供了完整的运行手册和脚本作为复现参考。

Abstract: Compact AI systems make local language-model experimentation increasingly accessible, yet practical evidence for multi-node training on desktop-class accelerators remains limited. This report presents a proof-of-concept deployment of distributed NanoChat pretraining across two NVIDIA DGX Spark systems, each with a GB10 Grace Blackwell system-on-chip and 128 GB of unified memory, administered remotely over a Tailscale mesh VPN and connected for training by a dedicated 200 Gb/s QSFP56 direct fiber link. PyTorch torchrun, DDP, and NCCL were configured with one process per node, a depth-20 NanoChat model, a local batch size of 32 per node, and a 2,048-token context, giving a global batch of 131,072 tokens per step. The run sustained a step time of about 69.4 s (about 1,890 tokens/s), processing about 653 million tokens over four days. We document link configuration, container setup, interface binding, a step-zero evaluation bug that triggered NCCL timeouts, checkpointing, and troubleshooting lessons, as a reproducibility reference for small labs.
  We also built a cybersecurity fine-tuning dataset from 77 CISA advisories (338 training, 37 validation conversations) and ran a 17-question held-out evaluation comparing a baseline SFT checkpoint against a CTI-augmented checkpoint with an Ollama-hosted LLM judge. CTI-specific categories improved while general-knowledge categories regressed, for a small overall change from 2.06 to 2.29 on a 0-10 scale. The same cluster supports a 400-level AI course (CS 426) and a query engine for CompTIA Security+ POGIL activities in CBS 255, showing modest local infrastructure can serve both research and teaching. The study establishes feasibility rather than a scaling-efficiency claim, since single-node throughput used for comparison was estimated, not measured under matched conditions. Runbook and scripts are available (see Code Availability).

</details>
